import { Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import Home from "./pages/Home";

export default function App() {
    return (
        <Routes>

            {/* ØµÙØ­Ø§Øª Ø¹Ù…ÙˆÙ…ÛŒ â€” Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø± Ù„Ø§Ú¯ÛŒÙ†â€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡ â†’ dashboard */}

                <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
                <Route path="/quiz" element={<Quiz />} />
                <Route path="/result" element={<Result />} />
            </Route>

            {/* ØµÙØ­Ø§Øª Ù…Ø­Ø§ÙØ¸Øªâ€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡ â€” Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø± Ù„Ø§Ú¯ÛŒÙ†â€ŒÙ†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ â†’ login */}

import { Navigate, Outlet } from "react-router-dom";
import { useContext } from "react";

import AuthContext from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function ProtectedRoute() {
    const { user, loading } = useContext(AuthContext);

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-cyan-400">
                Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨...
            </div>
        );
    }

    if (!user) {
        return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
    }

    return <Outlet />;
}

import { Navigate, Outlet } from "react-router-dom";
import { useContext } from "react";

import AuthContext from "../../context/AuthContext";

export default function PublicRoute() {
    const { user, loading } = useContext(AuthContext);

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-black text-cyan-400">
                Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ Ø¨Ø±Ø±Ø³ÛŒ Ø­Ø³Ø§Ø¨...
            </div>
        );
    }

    // Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø± Ù„Ø§Ú¯ÛŒÙ†â€ŒØ´Ø¯Ù‡ â†’ Ù†Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆÙ†Ù‡ ØµÙØ­Ø§Øª public Ø±Ùˆ Ø¨Ø¨ÛŒÙ†Ù‡

        return <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />;
    }

    return <Outlet />;
}

                    </div>
                ))}
            </div>

export default function DashboardContinue({ 
    currentSkill, 
    nextSkill, 
    allSkills, 
    skillProgress,

    progress,

}) {

                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-4">

                        {isInProgress ? "â³ Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ú¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ" : "ðŸš€ Ø´Ø±ÙˆØ¹ Ú©Ù†"}

export default function DashboardDailyGoal({ dailyProgress, setDailyProgress }) {
    const goal = 60;
    const percentage = Math.min(100, Math.floor((dailyProgress / goal) * 100));
    const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * 34;
    const offset = circumference - (percentage / 100) * circumference;

    return (
        <div className="p-6 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-yellow-500/5 to-orange-500/5 border border-yellow-500/20">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p className="text-zinc-500 text-sm">ðŸŽ¯ Ù‡Ø¯Ù Ø±ÙˆØ²Ø§Ù†Ù‡</p>
                    <h3 className="text-2xl font-black text-yellow-400">
                        {dailyProgress} Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡
                    </h3>
                    <p className="text-zinc-600 text-xs">Ø§Ø² {goal} Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡</p>
                </div>
                <div className="relative w-20 h-20">
                    <svg className="w-20 h-20 -rotate-90">
                        <circle
                            cx="40"
                            cy="40"
                            r="34"
                            stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.05)"
                            strokeWidth="8"
                            fill="none"
                        />
                        <circle
                            cx="40"
                            cy="40"
                            r="34"
                            stroke="url(#dailyGradient)"
                            strokeWidth="8"
                            fill="none"
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                            strokeDasharray={`${circumference} ${circumference}`}
                            strokeDashoffset={offset}
                            className="transition-all duration-700"
                        />
                        <defs>
                            <linearGradient id="dailyGradient" x1="0%" y1="0%" x2="100%" y2="0%">
                                <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#fbbf24" />
                                <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#f59e0b" />
                            </linearGradient>
                        </defs>
                    </svg>
                    <span className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold text-yellow-400">
                        {percentage}%
                    </span>
                </div>
            </div>
            {dailyProgress < goal && (
                <button 
                    onClick={() => setDailyProgress(prev => Math.min(60, prev + 15))}
                    className="w-full mt-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-yellow-400/10 border border-yellow-400/20 text-yellow-400 text-sm font-bold hover:bg-yellow-400/20 transition-colors"
                >
                    â± +Û±Ûµ Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ù‡
                </button>
            )}
        </div>
    );
}

export default function DashboardStats({ 
    roadmapProgress, 
    completedSkills, 
    allSkills, 
    inProgressSkills, 
    totalXP 
}) {
    const stats = [
        {
            label: "Ù¾ÛŒØ´Ø±ÙØª Ú©Ù„",
            value: `${roadmapProgress}%`,
            icon: "ðŸ“Š",
            color: "text-cyan-400",
            progress: roadmapProgress
        },
        {
            label: "Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§",
            value: `${completedSkills.length}/${allSkills.length}`,
            icon: "âœ…",
            color: "text-emerald-400",
            subtext: `${inProgressSkills.length} Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ú¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ`
        },
        {
            label: "XP Ú©Ù„",
            value: totalXP.toLocaleString(),
            icon: "â­",
            color: "text-yellow-400",
            subtext: `${completedSkills.length} Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øª Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„`
        },
        {
            label: "Ù…Ø±Ø­Ù„Ù‡ ÙØ¹Ù„ÛŒ",
            value: Math.floor(completedSkills.length / 3) + 1,
            icon: "ðŸŽ¯",
            color: "text-purple-400",
            subtext: `Ø§Ø² ${Math.ceil(allSkills.length / 3)} Ù…Ø±Ø­Ù„Ù‡`
        }
    ];

    const colors = {
        'text-cyan-400': 'border-cyan-400/20 hover:border-cyan-400/40',
        'text-emerald-400': 'border-emerald-400/20 hover:border-emerald-400/40',
        'text-yellow-400': 'border-yellow-400/20 hover:border-yellow-400/40',
        'text-purple-400': 'border-purple-400/20 hover:border-purple-400/40'
    };

    return (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            {stats.map((stat, index) => (
                <div 
                    key={index}
                    className={`p-6 rounded-3xl bg-gradient-to-br from-zinc-900/50 to-zinc-800/30 border ${colors[stat.color] || 'border-zinc-800'} transition-all group`}
                >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <p className="text-zinc-500 text-sm">{stat.label}</p>
                        <span className="text-xl">{stat.icon}</span>
                    </div>
                    <h2 className={`text-3xl font-black mt-3 ${stat.color}`}>
                        {stat.value}
                    </h2>
                    {stat.subtext && (
                        <p className="text-zinc-600 text-xs mt-1">{stat.subtext}</p>
                    )}
                    {stat.progress !== undefined && (
                        <div className="mt-3 h-1.5 bg-white/5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                            <div
                                className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 to-blue-400 rounded-full transition-all duration-700"
                                style={{ width: `${stat.progress}%` }}
                            />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            ))}
        </div>
    );
}

          const lineStartX = 120 + lineStartRadius * Math.cos(angle);
          const lineStartY = 120 + lineStartRadius * Math.sin(angle);
          const isAI = label === "AI";

          return (
            <g key={label} className={isAI ? classes.activePath : undefined}>
              <title>{label}</title>
              <line
                x1={lineStartX}
                y1={lineStartY}
                x2={x}
                y2={y}
                stroke={color}
                strokeWidth={isAI ? 0.55 : 0.3}
                strokeOpacity={isAI ? 0.34 : 0.12}
                strokeDasharray="1.5 3"
                strokeLinecap="round"
              />
              <circle cx={x} cy={y} r={size + 3.2} fill={color} opacity={0.035 * importance} />
              <circle cx={x} cy={y} r={size} fill="#020617" fillOpacity="0.92" stroke={color} strokeWidth={isAI ? 0.9 : 0.55} strokeOpacity={0.58 * importance} />
              <circle cx={x} cy={y} r={size * 0.32} fill={color} opacity={0.82 * importance} filter={isAI ? `url(#${ids.glow})` : undefined} />
              <text
                x={x}
                y={y + 1.7}
                textAnchor="middle"
                dominantBaseline="middle"
                fill="#e0f2fe"
                fontSize={shortLabel.length > 2 ? 3.3 : 4.2}
                fontWeight="800"
                fontFamily="'JetBrains Mono', 'Cascadia Code', monospace"
                opacity="0.78"
              >
                {shortLabel}
              </text>
            </g>
          );
        })}

        {/* Degree marks */}
        <g aria-hidden="true">
          {DEGREE_MARKS.map((_, index) => {
            const angle = (index / 36) * Math.PI * 2 - Math.PI / 2;
            const isPrimary = index % 9 === 0;
            const isSecondary = index % 3 === 0;
            const outerRadius = 93;
            const innerRadius = isPrimary ? 82 : isSecondary ? 86 : 89.5;

            return (
              <line
                key={index}
                x1={120 + innerRadius * Math.cos(angle)}
                y1={120 + innerRadius * Math.sin(angle)}
                x2={120 + outerRadius * Math.cos(angle)}
                y2={120 + outerRadius * Math.sin(angle)}
                stroke={isPrimary ? "#67e8f9" : "#64748b"}
                strokeWidth={isPrimary ? 1 : isSecondary ? 0.5 : 0.25}
                strokeOpacity={isPrimary ? 0.52 : isSecondary ? 0.22 : 0.11}
              />
            );
          })}

          {DIRECTIONS.map(({ label, angle, color }) => (
            <text
              key={label}
              x={120 + 58 * Math.cos(angle)}
              y={120 + 58 * Math.sin(angle) + 3}
              textAnchor="middle"
              fill={color}
              fontSize="8"
              fontWeight="800"
              fontFamily="monospace"
              opacity={label === "N" ? 0.92 : 0.68}
            >
              {label}
            </text>
          ))}
        </g>

        {/* Needle */}
        <g className={classes.needle}>
          <path
            d="M120 25 L130 107 L124 113 L120 104 L116 113 L110 107 Z"
            fill="#020617"
            opacity="0.24"
            transform="translate(1.2 1.8)"
          />
          <path
            d="M120 23 L130 107 L124 113 L120 103 L116 113 L110 107 Z"
            fill={`url(#${ids.northNeedle})`}
            opacity="0.96"
            filter={`url(#${ids.glow})`}
          />
          <path
            d="M116.2 91 L111.5 96 L116.2 101 M123.8 91 L128.5 96 L123.8 101"
            fill="none"
            stroke="#e0f2fe"
            strokeWidth="1"
            strokeLinecap="round"
            strokeLinejoin="round"
            opacity="0.55"
          />
          <line x1="120" y1="32" x2="120" y2="103" stroke="#ffffff" strokeWidth="0.65" strokeOpacity="0.42" />
          <path
            d="M120 198 L110.5 133 L116 126 L120 136 L124 126 L129.5 133 Z"
            fill={`url(#${ids.southNeedle})`}
            opacity="0.76"
          />
          <line x1="120" y1="190" x2="120" y2="136" stroke="#bfdbfe" strokeWidth="0.55" strokeOpacity="0.24" />
        </g>

        {/* Pivot + AI Core */}
        <g>
          <circle cx="120" cy="120" r="18" fill="#020617" fillOpacity="0.48" stroke="#67e8f9" strokeWidth="0.42" strokeOpacity="0.16" />
          <circle cx="120" cy="120" r="13" fill="#020617" stroke="#818cf8" strokeWidth="0.72" strokeOpacity="0.48" />
          <path
            d="M120 110 L128 115 L128 125 L120 130 L112 125 L112 115 Z"
            fill="#111827"
            stroke="#a78bfa"
            strokeWidth="0.65"
            strokeOpacity="0.52"
          />
          <g stroke="#c4b5fd" strokeWidth="0.35" strokeOpacity="0.3">
            <line x1="114" y1="116" x2="126" y2="116" />
            <line x1="114" y1="120" x2="126" y2="120" />
            <line x1="114" y1="124" x2="126" y2="124" />
          </g>
          <circle className={classes.centerPulse} cx="120" cy="120" r="7" fill={`url(#${ids.centerCore})`} />
          <circle cx="120" cy="120" r="2" fill="#ffffff" />
        </g>
      </svg>
    </>
  );
}

export default function ProgressBar({ currentQuestion, totalQuestions }) {

    const percentage = ((currentQuestion + 1) / totalQuestions) * 100;

    return (
        <div className="w-full">

            <div className="flex justify-between mb-2 text-sm text-zinc-400">

                <span>
                    Ø³Ø¤Ø§Ù„ {currentQuestion + 1}
                </span>

                <span>
                    {totalQuestions}
                </span>

            </div>

            <div className="w-full h-2 bg-zinc-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">

                <div
                    className="h-full bg-cyan-500 transition-all duration-500"
                    style={{ width: `${percentage}%` }}
                />

            </div>

        </div>
    );
}

import AnswerButton from "./AnswerButton";

export default function QuestionCard({
    question,
    selectedAnswer,
    onAnswer
}) {

    return (

        <div>

            <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-8">

                {question.title}

            </h1>

            <div className="space-y-4">

                {question.answers.map((answer, index) => (

                    <AnswerButton
                        key={index}
                        answer={answer}
                        selected={selectedAnswer === index}
                        onClick={() => onAnswer(index)}
                    />

                ))}

            </div>

        </div>

    );

}

export default function SkillCard({ skill, status, progress, onClick }) {

    const statusConfig = {
        completed: {
            glass: "bg-cyan-400/[0.04] backdrop-blur-lg border-cyan-400/25",
            dot: "bg-cyan-400 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(34,211,238,.6)]",
            ring: "border-cyan-400/50",
            text: "text-cyan-100",
            desc: "text-cyan-400/40",
            badge: "âœ“",
        },
        "in-progress": {
            glass: "bg-white/[0.03] backdrop-blur-lg border-white/[0.08]",
            dot: "bg-white shadow-[0_0_18px_rgba(255,255,255,.4)]",
            ring: "border-white/25",
            text: "text-white",
            desc: "text-zinc-400",
            badge: null,
        },
        available: {
            glass: "bg-white/[0.03] backdrop-blur-lg border-white/[0.08]",
            dot: "bg-white/70 shadow-[0_0_14px_rgba(255,255,255,.25)]",
            ring: "border-white/20",
            text: "text-white",
            desc: "text-zinc-400",
            badge: null,
        },
        locked: {
            glass: "bg-transparent border-white/[0.03] opacity-30",
            dot: "bg-white/10",
            ring: "border-white/5",
            text: "text-zinc-600",
            desc: "text-zinc-700",
            badge: "ðŸ”’",
        },
    };

    const config = statusConfig[status];

    return (
        <button
            onClick={() => status !== "locked" && onClick?.(skill)}
            disabled={status === "locked"}
            className={`
                w-full group relative overflow-hidden
                rounded-2xl border p-5
                flex items-center gap-5
                transition-all duration-300
                ${config.glass}
                ${status !== "locked"
                    ? "cursor-pointer hover:bg-cyan-400/[0.06] hover:border-cyan-400/30"
                    : "cursor-not-allowed"}
            `}
        >
            {/* Subtle inner glow on hover */}
            {status !== "locked" && (
                <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400/0 via-cyan-400/[0.02] to-cyan-400/0 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-500 pointer-events-none rounded-2xl" />
            )}

            {/* Left: Indicator */}
            <div className="relative flex-shrink-0">
                <div className={`
                    w-11 h-11 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center
                    border-2 transition-all duration-500
                    ${config.ring}
                    ${(status === "in-progress" || status === "available") && "group-hover:border-white/40"}
                `}>
                    {/* Inner dot */}
                    <div className={`
                        w-3 h-3 rounded-full transition-all duration-300
                        ${config.dot}
                        ${(status === "in-progress" || status === "available") && "group-hover:scale-110"}
                    `} />

                    {/* Progress arc for current skill */}
                    {status === "in-progress" && progress > 0 && (
                        <svg className="absolute inset-0 w-full h-full -rotate-90">
                            <circle
                                cx="22" cy="22" r="18"
                                fill="none"
                                stroke="rgba(34,211,238,0.25)"
                                strokeWidth="2"
                                strokeDasharray={`${progress * 1.13} 113`}
                                strokeLinecap="round"
                            />
                        </svg>
                    )}
                </div>

                {/* Badge */}
                {config.badge && (
                    <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1 text-xs">
                        {config.badge}
                    </span>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* Center: Info */}
            <div className="flex-1 text-left min-w-0">
                <h3 className={`font-bold text-base truncate transition-colors ${config.text}`}>
                    {skill.title}
                </h3>
                {skill.description && (
                    <p className={`text-xs truncate mt-0.5 transition-colors ${config.desc}`}>
                        {skill.description}
                    </p>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* Right: Status icon */}
            <div className="flex-shrink-0">
                {status === "completed" ? (
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-cyan-400/10 border border-cyan-400/20 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <svg className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-cyan-400" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={3}>
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" />
                        </svg>
                    </div>
                ) : (status === "in-progress" || status === "available") ? (
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full bg-white/[0.03] border border-white/[0.08] flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-500 group-hover:text-cyan-400 group-hover:border-cyan-400/30 transition-all">
                        <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={1.5}>
                            <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M4.5 12h15m0 0l-6.75-6.75M19.5 12l-6.75 6.75" />
                        </svg>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-full border border-white/5 flex items-center justify-center">
                        <div className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-white/10" />
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </button>

import { useContext } from "react";
import QuizContext from "../../context/QuizContext";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

export default function SkillModal({ skill, onClose }) {
    const { updateProgress } = useContext(QuizContext);
    const navigate = useNavigate();

    async function handleComplete() {
        if (!skill?.id) return;

        try {
            await updateProgress(skill.id, 100);
            onClose();
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Failed to complete skill:", error);
        }
    }

    if (!skill) return null;

    return (
        <div
            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-5"
            onClick={onClose}
        >
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xl" />

            <div
                className="relative w-full max-w-md bg-zinc-900/80 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-white/[0.08] rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-[0_0_60px_rgba(34,211,238,.08)]"
                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
                <div className="absolute top-0 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 w-3/4 h-px bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-cyan-400/40 to-transparent" />

                <button
                    onClick={onClose}
                    className="absolute top-4 right-4 w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-white/[0.04] border border-white/[0.06] flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-500 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/[0.08] hover:border-white/[0.12] transition-all duration-300"
                >
                    <svg
                        className="w-4 h-4"
                        fill="none"
                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                        stroke="currentColor"
                        strokeWidth={2}
                    >
                        <path
                            strokeLinecap="round"
                            strokeLinejoin="round"
                            d="M6 18L18 6M6 6l12 12"
                        />
                    </svg>
                </button>

                <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-cyan-400/[0.06] border border-cyan-400/15 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl mb-5">
                    {skill.icon || "ðŸ“˜"}

export default function SkillActionButton({
    getButtonText,
    isButtonDisabled,
    onProgressUpdate
}) {
    async function handleClick() {
        if (isButtonDisabled) return;

        try {
            await onProgressUpdate(100);
        } catch (error) {
            console.error("Failed to update progress:", error);
        }
    }

    return (
        <button
            onClick={handleClick}
            className={`
                px-10 py-4 rounded-2xl font-bold
                transition-all duration-300
                ${
                    isButtonDisabled
                        ? "bg-zinc-700/50 text-zinc-500 cursor-not-allowed"
                        : "bg-cyan-400 text-black hover:bg-cyan-300 active:scale-[0.98]"
                }
            `}
            disabled={isButtonDisabled}
        >
            {getButtonText()}
        </button>
    );
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

export default function SkillPrerequisites({ requires, progress }) {
    if (requires.length === 0) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-center">Ù¾ÛŒØ´â€ŒÙ†ÛŒØ§Ø²Ù‡Ø§</h2>
                <div className="bg-cyan-400/5 border border-cyan-400/10 rounded-2xl p-5 text-center">
                    <span className="text-2xl block mb-2">ðŸŽ‰</span>
                    <p className="text-cyan-300 font-bold">Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù¾ÛŒØ´â€ŒÙ†ÛŒØ§Ø²</p>
                    <p className="text-zinc-500 text-sm mt-1">Ù‡Ù…ÛŒÙ† Ø­Ø§Ù„Ø§ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØªÙˆÙ†ÛŒ Ø´Ø±ÙˆØ¹ Ú©Ù†ÛŒ</p>
                </div>
            </div>
        );
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-4">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-center">Ù¾ÛŒØ´â€ŒÙ†ÛŒØ§Ø²Ù‡Ø§</h2>
            <div className="space-y-2">
                {requires.map(item => {
                    const itemProgress = progress?.[item.id] || 0;
                    const isCompleted = itemProgress >= 100;
                    const isInProgress = itemProgress > 0 && itemProgress < 100;

                    let statusIcon = "ðŸ”’";
                    let statusText = "Ù‚ÙÙ„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡";
                    let statusClass = "text-red-400";

                    if (isCompleted) {
                        statusIcon = "âœ…";
                        statusText = "Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡";
                        statusClass = "text-emerald-400";
                    } else if (isInProgress) {
                        statusIcon = "ðŸš€";
                        statusText = `${Math.floor(itemProgress)}%`;
                        statusClass = "text-cyan-400";
                    }

                    return (
                        <Link
                            key={item.id}
                            to={`/skill/${item.id}`}
                            className="flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-2xl bg-white/[0.02] border border-white/5 hover:border-cyan-400/20 transition-all group"
                        >
                            <span className="text-xl">{item.icon || "ðŸ“˜"}</span>
                            <span className="flex-1 text-sm text-white group-hover:text-cyan-300 transition-colors">
                                {item.title}
                            </span>
                            <span className={`text-xs font-medium ${statusClass}`}>
                                {statusIcon} {statusText}
                            </span>
                        </Link>
                    );
                })}
            </div>
        </div>
    );
}

FILE: C:\Users\rose\desktop\devcompass\src\components\skills\SkillsCard.jsx

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import {
    HiOutlineLockClosed,
    HiArrowTrendingUp,
    HiCheckCircle,
} from "react-icons/hi2";

export default function SkillsCard({
    skill,
    status,
    value = 0,
    isLocked = false,
    prereqs,
    skillPath,
    hoveredSkill,
    setHoveredSkill,
}) {
    const progress = Math.min(
        100,
        Math.max(0, Number(value) || 0)
    );

    const isCompleted = status === "completed";
    const isInProgress = status === "in-progress";
    const isAvailable = status === "available";

    const canOpen =
        !isLocked &&
        !isCompleted;

    const handleMouseEnter = () => {
        setHoveredSkill?.(skill.id);
    };

    const handleMouseLeave = () => {
        setHoveredSkill?.(null);
    };

    return (
        <Link
            to={canOpen ? `/skill/${skill.id}` : "#"}
            onClick={(event) => {
                if (!canOpen) {
                    event.preventDefault();
                }
            }}
            onMouseEnter={handleMouseEnter}
            onMouseLeave={handleMouseLeave}
            className={`
                block
                p-4
                rounded-2xl
                border
                transition-all
                duration-200
                group

                ${
                    isLocked
                        ? `
                            border-zinc-800/50
                            bg-zinc-900/20
                            cursor-not-allowed
                            opacity-50
                        `
                        : isCompleted
                        ? `
                            border-emerald-400/10
                            bg-emerald-400/[0.02]
                            hover:border-emerald-400/20
                        `
                        : isInProgress
                        ? `
                            border-cyan-400/20
                            bg-cyan-400/[0.03]
                            hover:border-cyan-400/40
                        `
                        : `
                            border-white/5
                            bg-white/[0.02]
                            hover:border-cyan-400/20
                            hover:bg-cyan-400/[0.03]
                        `
                }
            `}
        >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">

                {/* ================================
                    ICON
                ================================= */}

                <div
                    className={`
                        w-10
                        h-10
                        rounded-xl
                        flex
                        items-center
                        justify-center
                        text-xl
                        flex-shrink-0

                        ${
                            isLocked
                                ? "bg-zinc-800/40"
                                : isCompleted
                                ? "bg-emerald-400/10"
                                : isInProgress
                                ? "bg-cyan-400/10"
                                : "bg-white/5"
                        }
                    `}
                >
                    {isLocked ? (
                        <HiOutlineLockClosed
                            className="text-zinc-600"
                            size={18}
                        />
                    ) : isCompleted ? (
                        <HiCheckCircle
                            className="text-emerald-400"
                            size={20}
                        />
                    ) : (

import { motion, AnimatePresence } from "framer-motion";
import { HiChevronDown } from "react-icons/hi2";
import SkillsCard from "./SkillsCard";

export default function SkillsCategory({
    category,
    isExpanded,
    onToggle,
    getSkillStatus,
    getPrerequisiteDetails,
    buildSkillPath,
    progress,
    hoveredSkill,
    setHoveredSkill,
}) {
    if (!category) return null;

    const stats = category.stats || {
        total: 0,
        completed: 0,
        inProgress: 0,
        locked: 0,
        available: 0,
        progress: 0,
    };

    const categorySkills = Array.isArray(category.skills)
        ? category.skills
        : [];

    const progressPercent = Math.min(
        100,
        Math.max(0, Number(stats.progress) || 0)
    );


    return (
        <div
            className={`
                rounded-3xl
                border
                transition-all
                duration-300
                ${category.borderColor || "border-white/10"}
                ${category.bgColor || "bg-white/[0.02]"}
                overflow-hidden
            `}
        >
            {/* ================================
                HEADER
            ================================= */}

            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onToggle(category.id)}
                aria-expanded={isExpanded}
                className="
                    w-full
                    flex
                    items-center
                    justify-between
                    p-5
                    md:p-6
                    hover:bg-white/[0.02]
                    transition-colors
                    text-right
                "
            >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 min-w-0">
                    {/* Category icon */}
                    <div
                        className={`
                            w-12
                            h-12
                            rounded-2xl
                            bg-gradient-to-br
                            ${category.color || "from-cyan-500 to-blue-500"}
                            flex
                            items-center
                            justify-center
                            text-2xl
                            flex-shrink-0
                        `}
                    >
                        {category.icon || "ðŸ“š"}
                    </div>

                    {/* Category info */}
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                        <h3 className="text-lg font-bold truncate">
                            {category.label}
                        </h3>

                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1">
                            <span
                                className={`
                                    text-xs
                                    font-mono
                                    ${category.textColor || "text-cyan-400"}
                                `}
                            >
                                {stats.completed} / {stats.total}
                            </span>

                            <div className="w-20 h-1.5 bg-white/10 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                                <div
                                    className={`
                                        h-full
                                        ${category.progressBg || "bg-cyan-400"}
                                        rounded-full
                                        transition-all
                                        duration-700
                                    `}
                                    style={{
                                        width: `${progressPercent}%`,
                                    }}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                                {progressPercent}%
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Header stats */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 flex-shrink-0">
                    {stats.completed > 0 && (
                        <span
                            className="
                                hidden
                                md:flex
                                items-center
                                gap-1
                                text-xs
                                bg-emerald-400/10
                                text-emerald-400
                                px-2
                                py-1
                                rounded-full
                            "
                        >
                            âœ… {stats.completed}
                        </span>
                    )}

                    {stats.inProgress > 0 && (
                        <span
                            className="
                                hidden
                                md:flex
                                items-center
                                gap-1
                                text-xs
                                bg-cyan-400/10
                                text-cyan-400
                                px-2
                                py-1
                                rounded-full
                            "
                        >
                            ðŸš€ {stats.inProgress}
                        </span>
                    )}

                    {stats.locked > 0 && (
                        <span
                            className="
                                hidden
                                md:flex
                                items-center
                                gap-1
                                text-xs
                                bg-zinc-700/30
                                text-zinc-500
                                px-2
                                py-1
                                rounded-full
                            "
                        >
                            ðŸ”’ {stats.locked}
                        </span>

                        animate={{
                            rotate: isExpanded ? 180 : 0,

                        transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                    >
                        <HiChevronDown
                            size={20}
                            className="text-zinc-500"
                        />
                    </motion.div>
                </div>
            </button>

            ================================= */}

                        âœ… {stats.completed}

                        ðŸ”’ {stats.locked}

                            opacity: 1,

                            duration: 0.3,

                        className="overflow-hidden"

                        <div className="px-5 pb-5 md:px-6 md:pb-6 space-y-2">

                                <p className="text-zinc-500 text-sm text-center py-6">
                                    Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±ØªÛŒ Ù¾ÛŒØ¯Ø§ Ù†Ø´Ø¯
                                </p>
                            ) : (
                                categorySkills.map((skill) => {

                                        progress?.[skill.id];

                                    const value = Math.min(
                                        100,
                                        Math.max(
                                            0,
                                            Number(rawValue) || 0
                                        )
                                    );
                                    

                                    const status =
                                        getSkillStatus(skill);

                                    const isLocked =
                                        status === "locked";

                                    const prereqs =
                                        getPrerequisiteDetails(
                                            skill
                                        );

                                    const skillPath =
                                        buildSkillPath(
                                            skill.id
                                        );

                                    return (
                                        <SkillsCard
                                            key={skill.id}
                                            skill={skill}
                                            status={status}
                                            value={value}
                                            isLocked={isLocked}
                                            prereqs={prereqs}
                                            skillPath={skillPath}
                                            hoveredSkill={
                                                hoveredSkill
                                            }
                                            setHoveredSkill={
                                                setHoveredSkill
                                            }
                                        />
                                    );
                                })
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </motion.div>
                )}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";

    HiPlay,

    HiLightBulb,
} from "react-icons/hi2";

export default function SkillsContinue({
    currentSkills = [],
    getSkillStatus,
    getPrerequisiteDetails,
    progress = {},
    hoveredSkill,
    setHoveredSkill,
}) {
    // ÙÙ‚Ø· Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒÛŒ Ú©Ù‡ ÙˆØ§Ù‚Ø¹Ø§Ù‹ Ø´Ø±ÙˆØ¹ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡â€ŒØ§Ù†Ø¯

    if (inProgressSkills.length === 0) {
        return null;

        <div className="space-y-4">
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <HiPlay
                    className="text-cyan-400"
                    size={22}
                />

                <h2 className="text-lg font-bold">
                    Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ú¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ

                </span>
            </div>

            {/* Skills */}

                {inProgressSkills.map((skill) => {
                    const status = getSkillStatus(skill);

                    const value = Math.min(
                        100,
                        Math.max(
                            0,
                            Number(progress?.[skill.id]) || 0
                        )
                    );

                        getPrerequisiteDetails(skill);

                    return (

                            onMouseEnter={() =>

                            }
                            onMouseLeave={() =>
                                setHoveredSkill?.(null)
                            }

// galaxy/CameraRig.jsx
import { useFrame } from "@react-three/fiber";
import { useRef } from "react";

export default function CameraRig() {
    const mouse = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });
    const smooth = useRef({ x: 0, y: 0 });

    if (typeof window !== "undefined") {
        window.onmousemove = (e) => {
            mouse.current.x = (e.clientX / window.innerWidth) * 2 - 1;
            mouse.current.y = -(e.clientY / window.innerHeight) * 2 + 1;
        };
    }

    useFrame(({ camera, clock }) => {
        const t = clock.getElapsedTime();

        smooth.current.x += (mouse.current.x - smooth.current.x) * 0.01;
        smooth.current.y += (mouse.current.y - smooth.current.y) * 0.01;

        const drift = Math.sin(t * 0.02) * 0.25;

        camera.position.x = smooth.current.x * 0.25 + drift;
        camera.position.y = smooth.current.y * 0.15;

        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);
    });

    return null;
}

export default function GalaxyCore() {

    const material = useMemo(() => {

        return new THREE.ShaderMaterial({

            vertexShader,
            fragmentShader,

            transparent: true,

            depthWrite: false,

            blending: THREE.NormalBlending

        });

    }, []);

    return (

        <mesh>

            <planeGeometry args={[6, 6]} />

            <primitive object={material} attach="material" />

        </mesh>

    );

}

import { useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { useFrame } from "@react-three/fiber";
import * as THREE from "three";

function makeNoise2D(seed = 1) {
    let s = seed % 233280;
    const rand = () => {
        s = (s * 9301 + 49297) % 233280;
        return s / 233280;
    };
    const p = [...Array(256)].map((_, i) => i);
    for (let i = 255; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(rand() * (i + 1));
        [p[i], p[j]] = [p[j], p[i]];
    }
    const perm = new Uint8Array(512);
    for (let i = 0; i < 512; i++) perm[i] = p[i & 255];

    const fade = (t) => t * t * t * (t * (t * 6 - 15) + 10);
    const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + t * (b - a);
    const grad = (hash, x, y) => {
        const h = hash & 3;
        const u = h < 2 ? x : y;
        const v = h < 2 ? y : x;
        return (h & 1 ? -u : u) + (h & 2 ? -v : v);
    };

    return (x, y) => {
        const X = Math.floor(x) & 255;
        const Y = Math.floor(y) & 255;
        x -= Math.floor(x);
        y -= Math.floor(y);
        const u = fade(x);
        const v = fade(y);
        const aa = perm[X + perm[Y]];
        const ab = perm[X + perm[Y + 1]];
        const ba = perm[X + 1 + perm[Y]];
        const bb = perm[X + 1 + perm[Y + 1]];
        return lerp(
            lerp(grad(aa, x, y), grad(ba, x - 1, y), u),
            lerp(grad(ab, x, y - 1), grad(bb, x - 1, y - 1), u),
            v
        );
    };
}

function fbm(noise, x, y, octaves = 6, lacunarity = 2.0, gain = 0.5) {
    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 0.5;
    let freq = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        value += amplitude * noise(x * freq, y * freq);
        freq *= lacunarity;
        amplitude *= gain;
    }
    return value;
}

// Ù†ÙˆÛŒØ²Ù ridged: Ù‚Ø¯Ø±Ù…Ø·Ù„Ù‚Ù fbmØŒ Ø®Ø·ÙˆØ·Ù Ø¨Ø§Ø±ÛŒÚ© Ùˆ Ø±Ø´ØªÙ‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²Ù‡ (Ø´Ø¨ÛŒÙ‡ Ø±Ú¯Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø¯ÙˆØ¯)
function ridgedFbm(noise, x, y, octaves = 6) {
    let value = 0;
    let amplitude = 0.5;
    let freq = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < octaves; i++) {
        const n = 1 - Math.abs(noise(x * freq, y * freq));
        value += amplitude * n * n; // Ù…Ø±Ø¨Ø¹ Ú©Ø±Ø¯Ù† Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø±Ø´ØªÙ‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§Ø±ÛŒÚ©â€ŒØªØ± Ùˆ ØªÛŒØ²ØªØ±
        freq *= 2.1;
        amplitude *= 0.5;
    }
    return value;
}

function parseRgba(str, fallback) {
    const m = str && str.match(/[\d.]+/g);
    if (!m || m.length < 3) return fallback;
    return [Number(m[0]), Number(m[1]), Number(m[2])];
}

export default function GalaxyNebula({
    position = [0, 0, -60],
    scale = [160, 160, 1],
    opacity = 0.4,
    color1 = "rgba(70,120,190,1)",
    color2 = "rgba(40,80,140,1)",
    rotationSpeed = 0.0003,
    seed,
}) {
    const spriteRef = useRef();
    const seedRef = useRef(
        seed ?? Math.floor(position[0] * 13 + position[1] * 7 + position[2] * 3 + 1000)
    );

    useFrame(() => {
        if (spriteRef.current) {
            spriteRef.current.material.rotation += rotationSpeed;
        }
    });

    const texture = useMemo(() => {
        const size = 1024;
        const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
        canvas.width = size;
        canvas.height = size;
        const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
        const imgData = ctx.createImageData(size, size);

        const noise = makeNoise2D(seedRef.current);
        const warpNoise = makeNoise2D(seedRef.current + 999);
        const [r1, g1, b1] = parseRgba(color1, [70, 120, 190]);
        const [r2, g2, b2] = parseRgba(color2, [40, 80, 140]);

        const scaleFactor = 2.6;
        const warpStrength = 0.9; // Ø´Ø¯Øª Ù¾ÛŒÚ†Ø´ Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù†Ù‡Ø› Ù‡Ø±Ú†ÛŒ Ø¨ÛŒØ´ØªØ±ØŒ Ø±Ø´ØªÙ‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ Ù…Ù†Ø­Ù†ÛŒâ€ŒØªØ± Ùˆ Ø¢Ø´ÙØªÙ‡â€ŒØªØ±

        for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) {
            for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
                const nx = (x / size) * scaleFactor;
                const ny = (y / size) * scaleFactor;

                // --- Domain Warping ---
                // Ù…Ø®ØªØµØ§Øª Ù†Ù…ÙˆÙ†Ù‡â€ŒØ¨Ø±Ø¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø±Ùˆ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ Ø§Ø² Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø³Ø¨Ù‡â€ŒÛŒ Ú†Ú¯Ø§Ù„ÛŒ Ø§ØµÙ„ÛŒØŒ Ø¨Ø§ ÛŒÙ‡ Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ‡ Ù†ÙˆÛŒØ²
                // Ø¬Ø¯Ø§Ú¯Ø§Ù†Ù‡ Ø¬Ø§Ø¨Ù‡â€ŒØ¬Ø§ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†ÛŒÙ…. Ø§ÛŒÙ† Ú©Ø§Ø± Ø®Ø·ÙˆØ· ØµØ§Ù/Ø¨Ù„ÙˆØ¨ÛŒ Ø±Ùˆ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø±Ø´ØªÙ‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ
                // Ù¾ÛŒÚ†â€ŒØ®ÙˆØ±Ø¯Ù‡ Ùˆ Ø¢Ø´ÙØªÙ‡â€ŒÛŒ Ø·Ø¨ÛŒØ¹ÛŒ ØªØ¨Ø¯ÛŒÙ„ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†Ù‡ (ØªÚ©Ù†ÛŒÚ© Ø§Ø³ØªØ§Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ø¯ Ø±Ù†Ø¯Ø± Ø§Ø¨Ø±/Ø¯ÙˆØ¯).
                const warpX = fbm(warpNoise, nx + 5.2, ny + 1.3, 4);
                const warpY = fbm(warpNoise, nx + 8.7, ny + 3.1, 4);

                const wx = nx + warpX * warpStrength;
                const wy = ny + warpY * warpStrength;

                const base = (fbm(noise, wx, wy, 6) + 1) / 2;
                const ridged = ridgedFbm(noise, wx * 1.8 + 40, wy * 1.8 + 40, 5);

                let density = base * 0.35 + ridged * 0.65;

                // ÙˆÛŒÙ†ÛŒØª Ù†Ø±Ù… Ù„Ø¨Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§
                const dx = x / size - 0.5;
                const dy = y / size - 0.5;
                const edge = 1 - Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) * 1.7);
                density *= Math.max(0, edge);

                // Ø¢Ø³ØªØ§Ù†Ù‡â€ŒÚ¯Ø°Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ù†Ø±Ù…: Ø¨ÛŒØ´ØªØ± Ø¨ÙˆÙ… Ø®Ø§Ù„ÛŒ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÙ…ÙˆÙ†Ù‡ØŒ ÙÙ‚Ø· Ø±Ø´ØªÙ‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø±ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù… Ø¯ÛŒØ¯Ù‡ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ´Ù†
                density = Math.pow(Math.max(0, density - 0.3), 1.8) * 2.6;
                density = Math.min(1, density);

                const mixT = base;
                const r = r1 * (1 - mixT) + r2 * mixT;
                const g = g1 * (1 - mixT) + g2 * mixT;
                const b = b1 * (1 - mixT) + b2 * mixT;

                const idx = (y * size + x) * 4;
                imgData.data[idx] = r;
                imgData.data[idx + 1] = g;
                imgData.data[idx + 2] = b;
                imgData.data[idx + 3] = Math.floor(density * 255 * opacity);
            }
        }

        ctx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
        const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
        tex.needsUpdate = true;
        return tex;
    }, [color1, color2, opacity]);

    return (
        <sprite ref={spriteRef} position={position} scale={scale}>
            <spriteMaterial
                map={texture}
                transparent
                depthWrite={false}
                blending={THREE.AdditiveBlending}
            />
        </sprite>
    );
}

import { useRef } from "react";
import { extend, useFrame } from "@react-three/fiber";
import * as THREE from "three";

import MeteorTrailMaterial from "./MeteorTrail";

extend({
    MeteorTrailMaterial,
});

export default function ShootingStar({
    minDelay = 5,
    maxDelay = 12,

    firstDelay = [1, 3],
}) {
    const groupRef = useRef();

    const trailRef = useRef();
    const materialRef = useRef();

    const headRef = useRef();
    const lightRef = useRef();

    const state = useRef({
        phase: "idle",

        timer: THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(
            firstDelay[0],
            firstDelay[1]
        ),

        dir: new THREE.Vector2(),

        speed: 100,

        length: 18,
    });

    function scheduleNext() {
        const s = state.current;

        s.phase = "idle";

        s.timer =
            THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(
                minDelay,
                maxDelay
            );
    }

    function startFlight() {
        const s = state.current;
        const g = groupRef.current;

        if (!g) return;

        // Ø´Ø±ÙˆØ¹ Ø§Ø² Ø¨ÛŒØ±ÙˆÙ† ØµÙØ­Ù‡
        g.position.set(
            -60,
            THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(
                18,
                35
            ),
            THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(
                -35,
                -15
            )
        );

        const angle =
            THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(
                -0.45,
                -0.2
            );

        s.dir.set(
            Math.cos(angle),
            Math.sin(angle)
        );

        s.speed =
            THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(
                90,
                140
            );

        s.length =
            THREE.MathUtils.randFloat(
                16,
                28
            );

        s.phase = "flying";

        g.rotation.z = angle;

        if (trailRef.current) {
            trailRef.current.scale.set(
                s.length,
                0.08,
                1
            );

            trailRef.current.position.set(
                -s.length * 0.5,
                0,
                0
            );
        }
    }

    useFrame((_, delta) => {
        const s = state.current;
        const g = groupRef.current;

        if (!g) return;

        if (s.phase === "idle") {
            s.timer -= delta;

            if (materialRef.current)
                materialRef.current.uOpacity = 0;

            if (headRef.current)
                headRef.current.material.opacity = 0;

            if (lightRef.current)
                lightRef.current.intensity = 0;

            if (s.timer <= 0)
                startFlight();

            return;
        }

        // Ø®Ø±ÙˆØ¬ Ø§Ø² ØµÙØ­Ù‡
        if (
            g.position.x > 65 ||
            g.position.y < -40
        ) {
            scheduleNext();
            return;
        }

        s.speed += delta * 8;

        const wobble =
            Math.sin(
                performance.now() * 0.01
            ) * 0.01;

        g.position.x +=
            (s.dir.x + wobble) *
            s.speed *
            delta;

        g.position.y +=
            s.dir.y *
            s.speed *
            delta;

        const opacity = 0.9;

        if (materialRef.current) {
            materialRef.current.uOpacity =
                opacity;

            // motion blur fake
            trailRef.current.scale.x =
                s.length *
                (1 + s.speed / 300);
        }

        if (headRef.current) {
            headRef.current.material.opacity =
                opacity;

            headRef.current.scale.setScalar(
                0.8 +
                    Math.sin(
                        performance.now() *
                            0.02
                    ) *
                        0.05
            );
        }

        if (lightRef.current) {
            lightRef.current.intensity =
                opacity * 4;
        }
    });

    return (
        <group ref={groupRef}>
            {/* Trail */}

            <mesh ref={trailRef}>
                <planeGeometry
                    args={[1, 1]}
                />

                <meteorTrailMaterial
                    ref={materialRef}
                    transparent
                    depthWrite={false}
                    blending={
                        THREE.AdditiveBlending
                    }
                />
            </mesh>

            {/* Head */}

            <mesh ref={headRef}>
                <sphereGeometry
                    args={[
                        0.07,
                        16,
                        16,
                    ]}
                />

                <meshBasicMaterial
                    color="#ffffff"
                    transparent
                    opacity={0}
                />
            </mesh>

            {/* Glow */}

            <pointLight
                ref={lightRef}
                color="#dbefff"
                intensity={0}
                distance={10}
                decay={2}
            />
        </group>
    );
}

import {
    useMemo,
    useRef,
    useEffect,
} from "react";

import {
    useFrame,
} from "@react-three/fiber";

import createStarGeometry from "./stars/StarGeometry";
import createStarMaterial from "./stars/StarMaterial";


export default function StarField({

    count = 4000,
    radius = 180,
    distribution = "sphere",
    opacity = 1,
    twinkle = true,

}) {


    const materialRef = useRef();



    const geometry = useMemo(
        () =>
            createStarGeometry({

                count,
                radius,
                distribution,

            }),

        [
            count,
            radius,
            distribution,
        ]
    );



    const material = useMemo(
        () => {

            const mat =
                createStarMaterial({

                    opacity,
                    twinkle,

                });


            materialRef.current = mat;


            return mat;

        },

        [
            opacity,
            twinkle,
        ]
    );



    useFrame(
        (_, delta) => {

            if(materialRef.current){

                materialRef.current
                .uniforms
                .uTime
                .value += delta;

            }

        }
    );



    useEffect(() => {


        return () => {

            geometry.dispose();

            material.dispose();

        };


    }, [
        geometry,
        material,
    ]);



    return (

        <points

            geometry={geometry}

            material={material}

        />

    );

}

import * as THREE from "three";
import { random } from "./StarUtils";

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Spherical density field (Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù¾Ø®Ø´ Ù†Ø§Ù…Ù†Ø¸Ù…/Ø·Ø¨ÛŒØ¹ÛŒØŒ Ù†Ù‡ ÛŒÚ©Ù†ÙˆØ§Ø®Øª Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„)
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Ø¨Ù‡â€ŒØ¬Ø§ÛŒ ÛŒÚ© Ù†ÙˆÛŒØ² Perlin Ø³Ù‡â€ŒØ¨Ø¹Ø¯ÛŒ Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ØŒ Ø§Ø² Ø¬Ù…Ø¹ Ú†Ù†Ø¯ "Ù…ÙˆØ¬" Ø±ÙˆÛŒ Ú†Ù†Ø¯ Ø¬Ù‡Øª Ø«Ø§Ø¨Øª
| Ø±Ù†Ø¯ÙˆÙ… Ø§Ø³ØªÙØ§Ø¯Ù‡ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†ÛŒÙ…. Ù†ØªÛŒØ¬Ù‡: Ù…ÛŒØ¯Ø§Ù† Ú†Ú¯Ø§Ù„ÛŒ Ù†Ø±Ù… Ùˆ Ú©Ù…â€ŒÙØ±Ú©Ø§Ù†Ø³ Ø±ÙˆÛŒ Ø³Ø·Ø­ Ú©Ø±Ù‡
| Ú©Ù‡ Ú†Ù†Ø¯ØªØ§ Ù†Ø§Ø­ÛŒÙ‡â€ŒÛŒ Ù¾Ø±ØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù…â€ŒØªØ± Ùˆ Ú†Ù†Ø¯ØªØ§ Ø®Ù„ÙˆØªâ€ŒØªØ± Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²Ù‡ - Ø¯Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Ø§ Ø­Ø³ ÙˆØ§Ù‚Ø¹ÛŒ
| Ø¢Ø³Ù…ÙˆÙ† Ø´Ø¨ Ú©Ù‡ Ù†Ù‚Ø§Ø·Ø´ Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„Ø§ ÛŒÚ©Ù†ÙˆØ§Ø®Øª Ø±Ù†Ø¯ÙˆÙ… Ù†ÛŒØ³ØªÙ†.
*/

const DENSITY_DIRS = [...Array(5)].map(() => {
    const v = new THREE.Vector3(
        Math.random() * 2 - 1,
        Math.random() * 2 - 1,
        Math.random() * 2 - 1
    ).normalize();
    return {
        dir: v,
        freq: 1.5 + Math.random() * 2.5,
        phase: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
    };
});

function densityAt(dirVector) {
    let sum = 0;
    for (const d of DENSITY_DIRS) {
        sum += Math.cos(dirVector.dot(d.dir) * d.freq * Math.PI + d.phase);
    }
    // Ù†Ø±Ù…Ø§Ù„â€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²ÛŒ Ø¨Ù‡ Ø¨Ø§Ø²Ù‡â€ŒÛŒ 0..1
    return (sum / DENSITY_DIRS.length + 1) / 2;
}

// Ø­Ø¯Ø§Ù‚Ù„ Ø§Ø­ØªÙ…Ø§Ù„ Ù¾Ø°ÛŒØ±Ø´ØŒ ØªØ§ Ù‡ÛŒÚ† Ù†Ø§Ø­ÛŒÙ‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„Ø§ Ø®Ø§Ù„ÛŒ Ù†Ø´Ù‡ (ÙÙ‚Ø· Ú©Ù…â€ŒØªØ±Ø§Ú©Ù…â€ŒØªØ±)
const DENSITY_FLOOR = 0.35;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Sphere Distribution
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

export function spherePosition(radius) {

    const theta = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;

    const phi = Math.acos(
        2 * Math.random() - 1
    );

    return new THREE.Vector3(

        radius *
            Math.sin(phi) *
            Math.cos(theta),

        radius *
            Math.cos(phi),

        radius *
            Math.sin(phi) *
            Math.sin(theta)

    );

}

/**
 * Ù…Ø«Ù„ spherePositionØŒ ÙˆÙ„ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ rejection sampling Ø±ÙˆÛŒ Ù…ÛŒØ¯Ø§Ù† Ú†Ú¯Ø§Ù„ÛŒ:
 * Ø¬Ù‡Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù†Ø²Ø¯ÛŒÚ© Ø¨Ù‡ "Ø®ÙˆØ´Ù‡â€ŒÙ‡Ø§" Ø´Ø§Ù†Ø³ Ø¨ÛŒØ´ØªØ±ÛŒ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø§Ù†ØªØ®Ø§Ø¨ Ø´Ø¯Ù† Ø¯Ø§Ø±Ù†.
 * Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ú¯Ø±ÙØªÙ† N Ø³ØªØ§Ø±Ù‡â€ŒÛŒ Ù†Ù‡Ø§ÛŒÛŒØŒ Ø§ÛŒÙ† ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹ Ø±Ùˆ ØªÙˆ ÛŒÙ‡ Ø­Ù„Ù‚Ù‡â€ŒÛŒ retry ØµØ¯Ø§ Ø¨Ø²Ù†
 * (Ù†Ù…ÙˆÙ†Ù‡ Ø¯Ø± StarGeometry.js).
 */
export function naturalSpherePosition(radius) {
    const dir = new THREE.Vector3(
        Math.random() * 2 - 1,
        Math.random() * 2 - 1,
        Math.random() * 2 - 1
    ).normalize();

    const density = densityAt(dir);
    const acceptProb = DENSITY_FLOOR + (1 - DENSITY_FLOOR) * density;

    if (Math.random() > acceptProb) {
        return null; // Ø±Ø¯ Ø´Ø¯ØŒ Ø¯ÙˆØ¨Ø§Ø±Ù‡ ØªÙ„Ø§Ø´ Ú©Ù†
    }

    return dir.multiplyScalar(radius);
}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Galaxy Distribution
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

export function galaxyPosition(radius) {

    const armCount = 4;

    const arm =
        Math.floor(
            Math.random() *
            armCount
        );

    const distance =
        Math.pow(
            Math.random(),
            1.8
        ) * radius;

    const angle =
        distance * 0.12 +
        (arm / armCount) *
        Math.PI * 2;

    const offset =
        random(
            -0.25,
            0.25
        );

    return new THREE.Vector3(

        Math.cos(
            angle + offset
        ) * distance,

        random(
            -6,
            6
        ),

        Math.sin(
            angle + offset
        ) * distance

    );

}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Milky Way Band
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

export function milkyWayPosition(radius) {

    const x =
        random(
            -radius,
            radius
        );

    const y =
        random(
            -8,
            8
        );

    const z =
        random(
            -25,
            25
        );

    return new THREE.Vector3(
        x,
        y,
        z
    );

}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Cluster Distribution
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

const clusters = [

    new THREE.Vector3(
        -40,
        12,
        -20
    ),

    new THREE.Vector3(
        35,
        -8,
        10
    ),

    new THREE.Vector3(
        -15,
        20,
        30
    ),

    new THREE.Vector3(
        25,
        5,
        -35
    )

];

export function clusterPosition() {

    const center =
        clusters[
            Math.floor(
                Math.random() *
                clusters.length
            )
        ];

    return new THREE.Vector3(

        center.x +
            random(
                -8,
                8
            ),

        center.y +
            random(
                -8,
                8
            ),

        center.z +
            random(
                -8,
                8
            )

    );

}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Dust
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

export function dustPosition(radius) {

    return new THREE.Vector3(

        random(
            -radius,
            radius
        ),

        random(
            -radius * 0.3,
            radius * 0.3
        ),

        random(
            -radius,
            radius
        )

    );

}

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Dispatcher
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
*/

export function createPosition(
    distribution,
    radius
) {

    switch(distribution){

        case "galaxy":
            return galaxyPosition(radius);

        case "milkyway":
            return milkyWayPosition(radius);

        case "cluster":
            return clusterPosition();

        case "dust":
            return dustPosition(radius);

        case "natural": {
            // Ú†ÙˆÙ† rejection sampling Ù…Ù…Ú©Ù†Ù‡ null Ø¨Ø±Ú¯Ø±Ø¯ÙˆÙ†Ù‡ØŒ ØªØ§ Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ÛŒØª ØªÙ„Ø§Ø´ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†ÛŒÙ…
            let pos = null;
            let attempts = 0;
            while (pos === null && attempts < 20) {
                pos = naturalSpherePosition(radius);
                attempts++;
            }
            return pos || spherePosition(radius);
        }

        default:
            return spherePosition(radius);

    }

}

import * as THREE from "three";

import {
    randomStarColor,
    randomStarSize,
    randomTwinkleSpeed,
    randomPhase,
    randomDepthLayer,
    randomBrightness,
} from "./StarUtils";

import {
    createPosition,
} from "./StarDistribution";


export default function createStarGeometry({

    count = 3000,

    radius = 180,

    distribution = "sphere",

} = {}) {


    const positions =
        new Float32Array(
            count * 3
        );


    const colors =
        new Float32Array(
            count * 3
        );


    const sizes =
        new Float32Array(
            count
        );


    const speeds =
        new Float32Array(
            count
        );


    const phases =
        new Float32Array(
            count
        );


    const brightness =
        new Float32Array(
            count
        );



    for(
        let i = 0;
        i < count;
        i++
    ){


        /*
        -----------------------
        Position
        -----------------------
        */


        const position =
            createPosition(
                distribution,
                radius
            );


        positions[i * 3] =
            position.x;


        positions[i * 3 + 1] =
            position.y;


        positions[i * 3 + 2] =
            position.z;




        /*
        -----------------------
        Color
        -----------------------
        */


        const color =
            randomStarColor();


        colors[i * 3] =
            color.r;


        colors[i * 3 + 1] =
            color.g;


        colors[i * 3 + 2] =
            color.b;




        /*
        -----------------------
        Size
        -----------------------
        */


        sizes[i] =
            randomStarSize();




        /*
        -----------------------
        Twinkle
        -----------------------
        */


        speeds[i] =
            randomTwinkleSpeed();


        phases[i] =
            randomPhase();




        /*
        -----------------------
        Brightness
        -----------------------
        */


        brightness[i] =
            randomBrightness();



        /*
        Layer info
        ÙØ¹Ù„Ø§ Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†ÛŒÙ…
        Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù†Ø³Ø®Ù‡ Ø¨Ø¹Ø¯ shader
        */

        randomDepthLayer();


    }




    const geometry =
        new THREE.BufferGeometry();



    geometry.setAttribute(

        "position",

        new THREE.BufferAttribute(
            positions,
            3
        )

    );


    geometry.setAttribute(

        "color",

        new THREE.BufferAttribute(
            colors,
            3
        )

    );


    geometry.setAttribute(

        "aSize",

        new THREE.BufferAttribute(
            sizes,
            1
        )

    );


    geometry.setAttribute(

        "aSpeed",

        new THREE.BufferAttribute(
            speeds,
            1
        )

    );


    geometry.setAttribute(

        "aPhase",

        new THREE.BufferAttribute(
            phases,
            1
        )

    );


    geometry.setAttribute(

        "aBrightness",

        new THREE.BufferAttribute(
            brightness,
            1
        )

    );



    return geometry;

}

import * as THREE from "three";

import vertexShader from "../shaders/star.vert?raw";
import fragmentShader from "../shaders/star.frag?raw";


export default function createStarMaterial({
    opacity = 1,
    twinkle = true,
} = {}) {


    const material =
        new THREE.ShaderMaterial({

            vertexShader,

            fragmentShader,


            transparent: true,


            depthWrite: false,


            vertexColors: true,


            blending:
                THREE.AdditiveBlending,


            uniforms: {

                uTime: {
                    value: 0,
                },


                uTwinkle: {
                    value: twinkle,
                },


                uOpacity: {
                    value: opacity,
                },

            },


        });



    return material;

}

import * as THREE from "three";
export const STAR_TYPES = [
    { name: "O", weight: 0.002, color: new THREE.Color("#9bbcff"), minBrightness: 0.9, maxBrightness: 1.15 },
    { name: "B", weight: 0.01,  color: new THREE.Color("#b9d0ff"), minBrightness: 0.85, maxBrightness: 1.1 },
    { name: "A", weight: 0.05,  color: new THREE.Color("#edf3ff"), minBrightness: 0.8, maxBrightness: 1.05 },
    { name: "F", weight: 0.12,  color: new THREE.Color("#fff8f2"), minBrightness: 0.75, maxBrightness: 1.0 },
    { name: "G", weight: 0.25,  color: new THREE.Color("#fff2c6"), minBrightness: 0.65, maxBrightness: 0.95 },
    { name: "K", weight: 0.28,  color: new THREE.Color("#ffd7a8"), minBrightness: 0.55, maxBrightness: 0.85 },
    { name: "M", weight: 0.288, color: new THREE.Color("#ffb56b"), minBrightness: 0.45, maxBrightness: 0.75 },
];
export function randomStarType() {

    const r = Math.random();

    let total = 0;

    for (const star of STAR_TYPES) {

        total += star.weight;

        if (r <= total) {

            return star;

        }

    }

    return STAR_TYPES.at(-1);

}

export function randomStarColor() {

    const star =
        randomStarType();

    const color =
        star.color.clone();

    const brightness =
        random(
            star.minBrightness,
            star.maxBrightness
        );

    color.multiplyScalar(
        brightness
    );

    return color;
}

export function randomBrightness() {
    const r = Math.random();

    if (r < 0.75)
        return random(1.0, 1.4); // Ø§Ú©Ø«Ø±ÛŒØª: Ù‚Ø§Ø¨Ù„ Ø¯ÛŒØ¯Ù†ØŒ Ù†Ù‡ Ù…Ø­Ùˆ

    if (r < 0.96)
        return random(1.4, 2.0);

    return random(2.0, 3.2); // Ù†Ø§Ø¯Ø±: ÙˆØ§Ù‚Ø¹Ø§ Ø¯Ø±Ø®Ø´Ø§Ù†
}
/**

export function randomSpherePosition(
    radius
) {

    const theta =
        Math.random() *
        Math.PI *
        2;

    const phi =
        Math.acos(
            THREE.MathUtils.randFloatSpread(
                2
            )
        );

    return new THREE.Vector3(

        radius *
            Math.sin(phi) *
            Math.cos(theta),

        radius *
            Math.cos(phi),

        radius *
            Math.sin(phi) *
            Math.sin(theta)

    );
}

/**
 * Ù„Ø±Ø²Ø´ Ø¬Ø²Ø¦ÛŒ

export function jitter(
    vector,
    amount
) {

    vector.x += random(
        -amount,
        amount
    );

    vector.y += random(
        -amount,
        amount
    );

    vector.z += random(
        -amount,
        amount
    );

    return vector;

}

/**
 * Ù„Ø§ÛŒÙ‡ Ø¹Ù…Ù‚
 */
export function randomDepthLayer() {

    const r =
        Math.random();

    if (r < 0.6)
        return "far";

    if (r < 0.9)
        return "mid";

    return "near";

}

import { createContext, useEffect, useState, useCallback } from "react";

const AuthContext = createContext();

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  // ========================================
  // LOAD SAVED SESSION
  // ========================================
  useEffect(() => {
    const savedUser = localStorage.getItem("devcompass_user");

    if (savedUser) {
      try {
        const parsedUser = JSON.parse(savedUser);
        setUser(parsedUser);
      } catch {
        localStorage.removeItem("devcompass_user");
      }
    }

    setLoading(false);
  }, []);

  const updateUser = useCallback((updatedData) => {
    setUser((previousUser) => {
      if (!previousUser && !updatedData) return null;

      const newUserData = {
        ...previousUser,
        ...updatedData,
      };

      localStorage.setItem("devcompass_user", JSON.stringify(newUserData));
      return newUserData;
    });
  }, []);
  // ========================================
  // ADD XP (Ø§ÙØ²Ø§ÛŒØ´ Ø§Ù…ØªÛŒØ§Ø² Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±)
  // ========================================
  // TODO: Ø¯Ø± ÙØ§Ø² Û´-Ûµ ÛŒÚ©Ù¾Ø§Ø±Ú†Ù‡â€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²ÛŒ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ´ÙˆØ¯.
  // Ø§ÛŒÙ† ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹ ÙØ¹Ù„Ø§Ù‹ Ø§Ø² context Ø®Ø§Ø±Ø¬ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ ØªØ§ Ú©Ø³ÛŒ Ø¨Ù‡â€ŒØ§Ø´ØªØ¨Ø§Ù‡
  // XP Ù…Ø­Ù„ÛŒ Ø±Ø§ Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† Ù‡Ù…Ú¯Ø§Ù…â€ŒØ³Ø§Ø²ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± ØªØºÛŒÛŒØ± Ù†Ø¯Ù‡Ø¯.
  const addXp = useCallback((amount = 50) => {
    setUser((previousUser) => {
      if (!previousUser) return null;

      const currentXp = Number(previousUser.xp || 0);
      const newXp = currentXp + amount;

      const updatedUser = {
        ...previousUser,
        xp: newXp,
      };

      localStorage.setItem("devcompass_user", JSON.stringify(updatedUser));

      return updatedUser;
    });
  }, []);

  // ========================================
  // REGISTER
  // ========================================
  async function register(username, email, password) {
    const response = await fetch(
      "http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/register.php",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ username, email, password }),
      }
    );

    if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
      throw new Error(data.message || "Ø«Ø¨Øªâ€ŒÙ†Ø§Ù… Ù†Ø§Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ Ø¨ÙˆØ¯");

  // LOGIN
  // ========================================
  async function login(email, password) {
    const response = await fetch(
      "http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/login.php",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, password }),
      }
    );

    const data = await response.json();

    if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
      throw new Error(data.message || "ÙˆØ±ÙˆØ¯ Ù†Ø§Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ Ø¨ÙˆØ¯");

  // UPDATE PROFILE
  // ========================================
  async function updateProfile(username, email) {
    if (!user?.id) {
      throw new Error("Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø± ÙˆØ§Ø±Ø¯ Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ Ø§Ø³Øª");
    }

      "http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/update-profile.php",

        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          user_id: Number(user.id),
          username,
          email,
        }),
      }
    );

    const data = await response.json();

    if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
      throw new Error(data.message || "ÙˆÛŒØ±Ø§ÛŒØ´ Ù¾Ø±ÙˆÙØ§ÛŒÙ„ Ù†Ø§Ù…ÙˆÙÙ‚ Ø¨ÙˆØ¯");

  // LOGOUT
  // ========================================
  function logout() {
    setUser(null);
    localStorage.removeItem("devcompass_user");
  }

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        loading,
        login,
        register,
        logout,
        updateProfile,
        updateUser,
        isAuthenticated: !!user,
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export default AuthContext;
