import {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  useCallback,
} from "react";

import AuthContext from "./AuthContext";
import generateRoadmap from "../engine/generateRoadmap";
import generateStages from "../engine/generateStages";

const QuizContext = createContext();

// ========================================
// NORMALIZE PROGRESS
// ========================================
function normalizeProgress(rawProgress) {
  if (!rawProgress || typeof rawProgress !== "object") {
    return {};
  }

  const normalized = {};

  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(rawProgress)) {
    normalized[Number(key)] = value;
  }

  return normalized;
}

export function QuizProvider({ children }) {
  const { user, updateUser } = useContext(AuthContext);

  // ========================================
  // STATE
  // ========================================
  const [result, setResult] = useState([]);
  const [roadmap, setRoadmap] = useState([]);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState({});

  // Skills State
  const [skills, setSkills] = useState([]);
  const [skillsLoading, setSkillsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [skillsError, setSkillsError] = useState(null);

  // ========================================
  // LOAD USER PROGRESS
  // ========================================
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user?.id) {
      setProgress({});
      return;
    }

    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadProgress() {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(
          `http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/progress.php?user_id=${user.id}`
        );

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
          throw new Error(data.message || "Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù¾ÛŒØ´Ø±ÙØª Ú©Ø§Ø±Ø¨Ø±");
        }

        if (!cancelled) {
          setProgress(normalizeProgress(data.data));
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          console.error("Failed to load user progress:", error);
          setProgress({});
        }
      }
    }

    loadProgress();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [user?.id]);

  // ========================================
  // LOAD ALL SKILLS
  // ========================================
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    async function loadSkills() {
      try {
        setSkillsLoading(true);
        setSkillsError(null);

        const response = await fetch(
          "http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/skills.php"
        );

        const data = await response.json();

        if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
          throw new Error(data.message || "Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù„ÛŒØ³Øª Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§");
        }

        if (!cancelled) {
          const skillsData = Array.isArray(data.data) ? data.data : [];
          setSkills(skillsData);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        if (!cancelled) {
          console.error("Failed to load skills:", error);
          setSkillsError(error.message || "Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø¯Ø±ÛŒØ§ÙØª Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§");
          setSkills([]);
        }
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) {
          setSkillsLoading(false);
        }
      }
    }

    loadSkills();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, []);

  // ========================================
  // GENERATE USER ROADMAP
  // ========================================
  // roadmap Ø§Ø² Ø±ÙˆÛŒ user.roadmap_goal Ùˆ skills Ù…Ø­Ø§Ø³Ø¨Ù‡ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒØ´Ù‡.
  // Ù†ÛŒØ§Ø²ÛŒ Ø¨Ù‡ fetch Ø§Ø² Ø³Ø±ÙˆØ± Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª â€” AuthContext Ù…Ù†Ø¨Ø¹ Ø­Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚ØªÙ‡.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (skillsLoading || skills.length === 0) {
      setRoadmap([]);
      return;
    }

    const goal = user?.roadmap_goal;

    if (!goal) {
      setRoadmap([]);
      return;
    }

    const roadmapSkills = generateRoadmap(goal, skills);
    const generatedRoadmap = generateStages(roadmapSkills);

    setRoadmap(generatedRoadmap);
  }, [user?.roadmap_goal, skills, skillsLoading]);

  // ========================================
  // ROADMAP PROGRESS CALCULATIONS
  // ========================================
  const progressData = useMemo(() => {
    const allRoadmapSkills = roadmap.flatMap((stage) => stage.skills || []);
    const totalSkills = allRoadmapSkills.length;

    const completedSkills = allRoadmapSkills.filter(
      (skill) => Number(progress?.[skill.id] || 0) >= 100
    );

    const completedSkillsCount = completedSkills.length;

    const roadmapProgress =
      totalSkills === 0
        ? 0
        : Math.round((completedSkillsCount / totalSkills) * 100);

    return {
      allSkills: allRoadmapSkills,
      totalSkills,
      completedSkills,
      completedSkillsCount,
      roadmapProgress,
    };
  }, [roadmap, progress]);

  // ========================================
  // UPDATE SKILL PROGRESS
  // ========================================
  const updateProgress = useCallback(
    async (skillId, value) => {
      const userId = Number(user?.id);
      const numericSkillId = Number(skillId);
      const numericValue = Number(value);

      if (!userId || !numericSkillId) {
        throw new Error(
          `Ø´Ù†Ø§Ø³Ù‡ Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª: user_id=${user?.id}, skill_id=${skillId}`
        );
      }

      if (
        Number.isNaN(numericValue) ||
        numericValue < 0 ||
        numericValue > 100
      ) {
        throw new Error(`Ù…Ù‚Ø¯Ø§Ø± Ù¾ÛŒØ´Ø±ÙØª Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø¹ØªØ¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Øª: ${value}`);
      }

      const response = await fetch(
        "http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/update-progress.php",
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            user_id: userId,
            skill_id: numericSkillId,
            progress: numericValue,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
        throw new Error(data.message || "Ø®Ø·Ø§ Ø¯Ø± Ø°Ø®ÛŒØ±Ù‡ Ù¾ÛŒØ´Ø±ÙØª");
      }

      // Update local progress state
      setProgress((previous) => ({
        ...previous,
        [numericSkillId]: numericValue,
      }));

      // Update user state if API returned new user info
      if (data.data?.user) {
        updateUser(data.data.user);
      }

      return data;
    },
    [user?.id, updateUser]
  );

  // ========================================
  // MEMOIZED CONTEXT VALUE
  // ========================================
  const contextValue = useMemo(
    () => ({
      result,
      setResult,
      skills,
      setSkills,
      skillsLoading,
      skillsError,
      roadmap,
      setRoadmap,
      progress,
      setProgress,
      allSkills: progressData.allSkills,
      totalSkills: progressData.totalSkills,
      completedSkills: progressData.completedSkills,
      completedSkillsCount: progressData.completedSkillsCount,
      roadmapProgress: progressData.roadmapProgress,
      updateProgress,
    }),
    [
      result,
      skills,
      skillsLoading,
      skillsError,
      roadmap,
      progress,
      progressData,
      updateProgress,
    ]
  );

  return (
    <QuizContext.Provider value={contextValue}>
      {children}
    </QuizContext.Provider>
  );
}

// ========================================
// CUSTOM HOOK
// ========================================
export function useQuiz() {
  const context = useContext(QuizContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useQuiz Ø¨Ø§ÛŒØ¯ Ø¯Ø§Ø®Ù„ QuizProvider Ø§Ø³ØªÙØ§Ø¯Ù‡ Ø´ÙˆØ¯");
  }
  return context;
}

export default QuizContext;

export default function calculatePercentages(scores) {

    const total = Object.values(scores).reduce(
        (sum, value) => sum + value,
        0
    );

    const percentages = {};

    for (const [category, value] of Object.entries(scores)) {

        percentages[category] = Math.round((value / total) * 100);

    }

    return percentages;

}


============================================================
FILE: C:\Users\rose\desktop\devcompass\src\engine\calculateScore.js
============================================================

export default function calculateScore(questions, answers) {

    const scores = {};

    for (const question of questions) {

        const answerIndex = answers[question.id];

        if (answerIndex === undefined) continue;

        const selectedAnswer = question.answers[answerIndex];

        for (const [category, value] of Object.entries(selectedAnswer.score)) {

            scores[category] = (scores[category] || 0) + value;

        }

    }

    return scores;

}

FILE: C:\Users\rose\desktop\devcompass\src\engine\generateRoadmap.js
============================================================

export default function generateRoadmap(goal, skills) {
    const roadmap = [];
    const visited = new Set();

    function addSkill(skillId) {
        if (visited.has(skillId)) {
            return;
        }

        visited.add(skillId);

        const skill = skills.find(
            (item) => Number(item.id) === Number(skillId)
        );

        if (!skill) {
            return;
        }

        const requires = Array.isArray(skill.requires)
            ? skill.requires
            : [];

        // Ø§ÙˆÙ„ Ù¾ÛŒØ´â€ŒÙ†ÛŒØ§Ø²Ù‡Ø§
        requires.forEach((requirement) => {
            addSkill(requirement);
        });

        // Ø¨Ø¹Ø¯ Ø®ÙˆØ¯ Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øª
        roadmap.push(skill);
    }

    const goalSkills = skills.filter(
        (skill) =>
            Array.isArray(skill.goals) &&
            skill.goals.includes(goal)
    );

    goalSkills.forEach((skill) => {
        addSkill(skill.id);
    });

    return roadmap;
}

FILE: C:\Users\rose\desktop\devcompass\src\engine\generateStages.js
============================================================

export default function generateStages(roadmap) {

    const stages = {};

    roadmap.forEach(skill => {

        if (!stages[skill.stage]) {

            stages[skill.stage] = [];

        }

        stages[skill.stage].push(skill);

    });

    return Object
        .entries(stages)
        .map(([stage, skills]) => ({

            stage: Number(stage),

            skills

        }))
        .sort(
            (a, b) =>
                a.stage - b.stage
        );

}

// ========================================
// SKILL STATUS
// ========================================
//
// Ø§ÛŒÙ† ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹ ØªÙ†Ù‡Ø§ Ù…Ù†Ø¨Ø¹ Ø­Ù‚ÛŒÙ‚Øª Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ ÙˆØ¶Ø¹ÛŒØª ÛŒÚ© Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øª Ø§Ø³Øª.
//
// Ù¾Ø§Ø±Ø§Ù…ØªØ±Ù‡Ø§:
//   skill          â†’ Ø¢Ø¨Ø¬Ú©Øª Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øª (Ø´Ø§Ù…Ù„ id Ùˆ requires)
//   progress       â†’ Ø¢Ø¨Ø¬Ú©Øª progress (Ú©Ù„ÛŒØ¯Ù‡Ø§ Ø¹Ø¯Ø¯ÛŒ)
//   orderedSkills  â†’ Ø¢Ø±Ø§ÛŒÙ‡â€ŒÛŒ Ù…Ø±ØªØ¨ Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§ (Ø§Ø®ØªÛŒØ§Ø±ÛŒ)
//                    Ø§Ú¯Ù‡ Ø¯Ø§Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø´Ù‡ØŒ Ù…Ù†Ø·Ù‚ linear (Ù‚Ø¯Ù… Ø¨Ù‡ Ù‚Ø¯Ù…)
//                    Ø§Ú¯Ù‡ Ù†Ø¯Ø§Ø¯Ù‡ Ø¨Ø´Ù‡ØŒ fallback Ø¨Ù‡ pre-req
//
// Ø®Ø±ÙˆØ¬ÛŒ:
//   "completed"    â†’ progress >= 100
//   "in-progress"  â†’ 0 < progress < 100
//   "available"    â†’ progress = 0 Ùˆ Ù†ÙˆØ¨ØªØ´Ù‡
//   "locked"       â†’ progress = 0 Ùˆ Ù†ÙˆØ¨ØªØ´ Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª

export function getSkillStatus(skill, progress = {}, orderedSkills = []) {
    // ----------------------------------------
    // 1. Validation
    // ----------------------------------------

    if (!skill) {
        return "locked";
    }

    // ----------------------------------------
    // 2. Progress
    // ----------------------------------------

    const skillId = Number(skill.id);
    const value = Number(progress?.[skillId]) || 0;

    // ----------------------------------------
    // 3. Completed
    // ----------------------------------------

    if (value >= 100) {
        return "completed";
    }

    // ----------------------------------------
    // 4. In Progress
    // ----------------------------------------

    if (value > 0) {
        return "in-progress";
    }

    // ----------------------------------------
    // 5. Fallback: pre-req based (Ø¨Ø¯ÙˆÙ† orderedSkills)
    // ----------------------------------------

    if (!Array.isArray(orderedSkills) || orderedSkills.length === 0) {
        const requires = Array.isArray(skill.requires)
            ? skill.requires
            : [];

        if (requires.length === 0) {
            return "available";
        }

        const hasUncompletedRequired = requires.some((reqId) => {
            const reqValue = Number(progress?.[Number(reqId)]) || 0;
            return reqValue < 100;
        });

        return hasUncompletedRequired ? "locked" : "available";
    }

    // ----------------------------------------
    // 6. Linear: Ø¨Ø± Ø§Ø³Ø§Ø³ ØªØ±ØªÛŒØ¨ roadmap
    // ----------------------------------------

    const index = orderedSkills.findIndex(
        (s) => Number(s.id) === skillId
    );

    // skill ØªÙˆÛŒ Ù„ÛŒØ³Øª Ù†ÛŒØ³Øª â†’ fallback
    if (index < 0) {
        const requires = Array.isArray(skill.requires)
            ? skill.requires
            : [];

        if (requires.length === 0) {
            return "available";
        }

        const hasUncompletedRequired = requires.some((reqId) => {
            const reqValue = Number(progress?.[Number(reqId)]) || 0;
            return reqValue < 100;
        });

        return hasUncompletedRequired ? "locked" : "available";
    }

    // Ø§ÙˆÙ„ÛŒÙ† skill ØªÙˆÛŒ Ù„ÛŒØ³Øª â†’ available
    if (index === 0) {
        return "available";
    }

    // Ú†Ú© Ú©Ù† Ù‡Ù…Ù‡â€ŒÛŒ skillÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ Ù‚Ø¨Ù„ÛŒ Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø´Ø¯Ù†
    const allPreviousCompleted = orderedSkills
        .slice(0, index)
        .every((prev) => {
            const prevValue = Number(progress?.[Number(prev.id)]) || 0;
            return prevValue >= 100;
        });

    return allPreviousCompleted ? "available" : "locked";
}

export default function sortByPercentage(percentages) {

    return Object.entries(percentages)

        .map(([id, percentage]) => ({
            id,
            percentage
        }))

        .sort((a, b) => b.percentage - a.percentage);

}

import { useContext } from "react";
import { useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import QuizContext from "../context/QuizContext";
import { getSkillStatus } from "../engine/skillStatus";


// ========================================
// STATUS LABELS
// ========================================
//
// Ø§ÛŒÙ† mapØŒ Ø®Ø±ÙˆØ¬ÛŒ getSkillStatus Ø±Ùˆ Ø¨Ù‡ label Ùˆ className
// Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ù†Ù…Ø§ÛŒØ´ ØªØ¨Ø¯ÛŒÙ„ Ù…ÛŒâ€ŒÚ©Ù†Ù‡.

// Ú†Ø±Ø§ Ø§ÛŒÙ†Ø¬Ø§ Ùˆ Ù†Ù‡ ØªÙˆÛŒ engineØŸ Ú†ÙˆÙ† label Ùˆ className
// Ù…Ø±Ø¨ÙˆØ· Ø¨Ù‡ UI Ù‡Ø³ØªÙ†ØŒ Ù†Ù‡ business logic.


const STATUS_LABELS = {
    completed: {
        label: "Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ âœ…",
        className:
            "bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400",
    },
    "in-progress": {
        label: "Ø¯Ø± Ø­Ø§Ù„ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ú¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ ðŸš€",
        className:
            "bg-cyan-500/10 border-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400",
    },
    available: {
        label: "Ø¢Ù…Ø§Ø¯Ù‡ Ø´Ø±ÙˆØ¹ âœ¨",
        className:
            "bg-green-500/10 border-green-500/20 text-green-400",
    },
    locked: {
        label: "Ù‚ÙÙ„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡ ðŸ”’",
        className:
            "bg-red-500/10 border-red-500/20 text-red-400",
    },
};

export function useSkillData() {
    const { id } = useParams();

    const {
        roadmap,
        progress,
        updateProgress,
        allSkills,
        totalSkills,
        roadmapProgress,
    } = useContext(QuizContext);

    // --------------------------------
    // Current skill
    // --------------------------------

    const skill = allSkills.find(
        (item) => String(item.id) === String(id)
    );
    const orderedSkills = roadmap.flatMap((stage) => stage.skills || []);


    const skillProgress = Number(
        progress?.[skill?.id] || 0
    );

    // --------------------------------
    // Difficulty
    // --------------------------------

    const difficultyLabels = {
        1: "Ø¢Ø³ÙˆÙ†",
        2: "Ù…ØªÙˆØ³Ø·",
        3: "Ø³Ø®Øª",
        4: "Ø®ÛŒÙ„ÛŒ Ø³Ø®Øª",
        5: "Ø§ÙØ³Ø§Ù†Ù‡â€ŒØ§ÛŒ",
    };

    const diffText =
        difficultyLabels[Number(skill?.difficulty)] ||
        "Ù†Ø§Ù…Ø´Ø®Øµ";

    // --------------------------------
    // Overall roadmap progress
    // --------------------------------

    const overallProgress = roadmapProgress;

    // --------------------------------
    // Prerequisites
    // --------------------------------

    const requires = allSkills.filter((item) =>
        (skill?.requires || [])
            .map(Number)
            .includes(Number(item.id))
    );

    // --------------------------------
    // Check prerequisite
    // --------------------------------

    const hasCompletedPrerequisites = (targetSkill) => {
        const requiredSkills = allSkills.filter((item) =>
            (targetSkill?.requires || [])
                .map(Number)
                .includes(Number(item.id))
        );

        return requiredSkills.every(
            (requiredSkill) =>
                Number(progress?.[requiredSkill.id] || 0) >= 100
        );
    };

    // --------------------------------
    // Current and next stage
    // --------------------------------

    const currentStage =
        roadmap.find((stage) =>
            stage.skills?.some(
                (item) => String(item.id) === String(skill?.id)
            )
        ) || null;

    const currentStageIndex = currentStage
        ? roadmap.indexOf(currentStage)
        : -1;

    const nextStage =
        currentStageIndex >= 0
            ? roadmap[currentStageIndex + 1] || null
            : null;

    // --------------------------------
    // Next skills in current stage
    // --------------------------------

    const currentStageSkills =
        currentStage?.skills || [];

    const currentSkillIndex =
        currentStageSkills.findIndex(
            (item) => String(item.id) === String(skill?.id)
        );

    const nextSkillsInStage =
        currentSkillIndex >= 0
            ? currentStageSkills.slice(
                currentSkillIndex + 1
            )
            : [];

    // --------------------------------
    // Suggested skills
    // --------------------------------

    const getNextSuggestedSkills = () => {
        const suggested = [];

        // First: skills later in current stage
        for (const candidate of nextSkillsInStage) {
            if (suggested.length >= 2) break;

            const candidateProgress =
                Number(progress?.[candidate.id] || 0);

            if (
                candidateProgress === 0 &&
                hasCompletedPrerequisites(candidate)
            ) {
                suggested.push(candidate);
            }
        }

        // Then: skills from next stage
        if (suggested.length < 2 && nextStage) {
            const nextStageSkills =
                nextStage.skills || [];

            for (const candidate of nextStageSkills) {
                if (suggested.length >= 2) break;

                const candidateProgress =
                    Number(progress?.[candidate.id] || 0);

                if (
                    candidateProgress === 0 &&
                    hasCompletedPrerequisites(candidate)
                ) {
                    suggested.push(candidate);
                }
            }
        }

        return suggested;
    };

    const suggestedSkills =
        getNextSuggestedSkills();

    // --------------------------------
    // Skill status (from engine)
    // --------------------------------

    const statusKey = getSkillStatus(skill, progress, orderedSkills);

    const status = STATUS_LABELS[statusKey];

    // --------------------------------
    // Button
    // --------------------------------

    const getButtonText = () => {
        if (skillProgress >= 100) {
            return "âœ… Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù„ Ø´Ø¯Ù‡";
        }

        if (skillProgress > 0) {
            return "â³ Ø§Ø¯Ø§Ù…Ù‡ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ú¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ";
        }

        return "ðŸš€ Ø´Ø±ÙˆØ¹ ÛŒØ§Ø¯Ú¯ÛŒØ±ÛŒ";
    };

    const isButtonDisabled =
        skillProgress >= 100 ||
        statusKey === "locked";

    // --------------------------------
    // Estimate end date
    // --------------------------------

    const estimateEndDate = () => {
        if (!skill) return null;

        if (skillProgress >= 100) {
            return "ØªÙ…ÙˆÙ… Ø´Ø¯Ù‡! ðŸŽ‰";
        }

        const skillTime = Number(skill.time || 0);

        const remainingDays = Math.max(
            0,
            skillTime -
            skillTime *
            (skillProgress / 100)
        );

        const dailyHours = 2;

        const totalHours =
            remainingDays * dailyHours;

        if (remainingDays <= 0) {
            return "Ø§Ù…Ø±ÙˆØ² ØªÙ…ÙˆÙ… Ù…ÛŒØ´Ù‡! ðŸ”¥";
        }

        if (remainingDays < 1) {
            return `${Math.ceil(
                totalHours
            )} Ø³Ø§Ø¹Øª Ø¯ÛŒÚ¯Ù‡ â°`;
        }

        if (remainingDays < 7) {
            return `${Math.ceil(
                remainingDays
            )} Ø±ÙˆØ² Ø¯ÛŒÚ¯Ù‡ ðŸ“…`;
        }

        if (remainingDays < 30) {
            return `${Math.ceil(
                remainingDays / 7
            )} Ù‡ÙØªÙ‡ Ø¯ÛŒÚ¯Ù‡ ðŸ“†`;
        }

        return `${Math.ceil(
            remainingDays / 30
        )} Ù…Ø§Ù‡ Ø¯ÛŒÚ¯Ù‡ ðŸ—“ï¸`;
    };

    // --------------------------------
    // Update progress
    // --------------------------------

    const handleProgressUpdate = async (value) => {
        if (!skill?.id) return;

        await updateProgress(
            skill.id,
            value
        );
    };

    // --------------------------------
    // Return
    // --------------------------------

    return {
        id,

        // Current skill
        skill,
        skillProgress,
        diffText,

        // Roadmap
        roadmap,
        allSkills,
        totalSkills,

        // Progress
        progress,
        overallProgress,

        // Prerequisites
        requires,
        hasCompletedPrerequisites,

        // Stages
        currentStage,
        currentStageIndex,
        nextStage,
        nextSkillsInStage,

        // Suggestions
        suggestedSkills,

        // Status
        status,
        statusKey,

        // Button
        getButtonText,
        isButtonDisabled,

        // Estimate
        estimateEndDate,

        // Action
        handleProgressUpdate,
    };
}

import {
    useState,
    useContext,
    useMemo,
    useCallback,
} from "react";

import QuizContext from "../context/QuizContext";
import { CATEGORIES } from "../constants/categories";
import { getSkillStatus } from "../engine/skillStatus";

export function useSkills() {
    const {
        skills,
        skillsLoading,
        skillsError,
        progress,
    } = useContext(QuizContext);

    // ========================================
    // STATE
    // ========================================

    const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] =
        useState("");

        useState("progress");

        useState(
            CATEGORIES.reduce(
                (acc, category) => ({
                    ...acc,

                                                        b.xp
                                                    ) ||
                                                    0
                                                ) -
                                                (
                                                    Number(
                                                        a.xp
                                                    ) ||
                                                    0
                                                )
                                            );

                                        case "name":
                                            return String(
                                                a.title ||
                                                    ""
                                            ).localeCompare(
                                                String(
                                                    b.title ||
                                                        ""
                                                )
                                            );

                                        default:
                                            return 0;
                                    }
                                }
                            );

                        // -------------------------
                        // Stats
                        // -------------------------

                        const completed =
                            sorted.filter(
                                (
                                    skill
                                ) =>
                                    getSkillStatusForSkill(
                                        skill
                                    ) ===
                                    "completed"
                            ).length;

                        const inProgress =
                            sorted.filter(
                                (
                                    skill
                                ) =>
                                    getSkillStatusForSkill(
                                        skill
                                    ) ===
                                    "in-progress"
                            ).length;

                        const locked =
                            sorted.filter(
                                (
                                    skill
                                ) =>
                                    getSkillStatusForSkill(
                                        skill
                                    ) ===
                                    "locked"
                            ).length;

                        const available =
                            sorted.length -
                            completed -
                            inProgress -
                            locked;

                        const categoryProgress =
                            sorted.length >
                            0
                                ? Math.round(
                                      (
                                          completed /
                                          sorted.length
                                      ) *
                                          100
                                  )
                                : 0;

                        return {
                            ...category,

                            skills:
                                sorted,

                            stats: {
                                total:
                                    sorted.length,

                                completed,

                                inProgress,

                                locked,

                                available,

                                progress:
                                    categoryProgress,
                            },
                        };
                    }
                );

            return result.filter(
                (category) =>
                    category.skills
                        .length > 0
            );
        }, [
            allSkills,
            progress,
            searchTerm,
            sortBy,
            getSkillStatusForSkill,
        ]);

    // ========================================
    // GLOBAL STATS
    // ========================================

    const globalStats =
        useMemo(() => {
            const completed =
                allSkills.filter(
                    (skill) =>
                        getSkillStatusForSkill(
                            skill
                        ) ===
                        "completed"
                ).length;

            const inProgress =
                allSkills.filter(
                    (skill) =>
                        getSkillStatusForSkill(
                            skill
                        ) ===
                        "in-progress"
                ).length;

            const total =
                allSkills.length;

            const earnedXP =
                allSkills
                    .filter(
                        (skill) =>
                            getSkillStatusForSkill(
                                skill
                            ) ===
                            "completed"
                    )
                    .reduce(
                        (
                            acc,
                            skill
                        ) =>
                            acc +
                            (
                                Number(
                                    skill.xp
                                ) ||
                                Number(
                                    skill.difficulty
                                ) *
                                    100 ||
                                100
                            ),
                        0
                    );

            const totalXP =
                allSkills.reduce(
                    (
                        acc,
                        skill
                    ) =>
                        acc +
                        (
                            Number(
                                skill.xp
                            ) ||
                            Number(
                                skill.difficulty
                            ) *
                                100 ||
                            100
                        ),
                    0
                );

            const level =
                Math.floor(
                    completed / 3
                ) + 1;

            return {
                completed,
                inProgress,
                total,
                earnedXP,
                totalXP,
                level,
            };
        }, [
            allSkills,
            getSkillStatusForSkill,
        ]);

    // ========================================
    // TOGGLE CATEGORY
    // ========================================

    const toggleCategory =
        useCallback(
            (categoryId) => {
                setExpandedCategories(
                    (previous) => ({
                        ...previous,

                        [categoryId]:
                            !previous[
                                categoryId
                            ],
                    })
                );
            },
            []
        );

    // ========================================
    // RETURN
    // ========================================

    return {
        // --------------------------------
        // Loading / Error
        // --------------------------------

        loading: skillsLoading,
        error: skillsError,

        // --------------------------------
        // State
        // --------------------------------

        searchTerm,
        setSearchTerm,

        sortBy,
        setSortBy,

        expandedCategories,

        hoveredSkill,
        setHoveredSkill,

        // --------------------------------
        // Data
        // --------------------------------

        allSkills,
        skillsMap,

        // --------------------------------
        // User Progress
        // --------------------------------

        progress,

        // --------------------------------
        // Functions
        // --------------------------------

        // Ø§ÛŒÙ† wrapperØŒ Ø¨Ø±Ø§ÛŒ Ø³Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø§Ø±ÛŒ Ø¨Ø§ Ú©Ø§Ù…Ù¾ÙˆÙ†Ù†Øªâ€ŒÙ‡Ø§ÛŒ ÙØ¹Ù„ÛŒ Ø§Ø³Øª.
        // Ø®ÙˆØ¯ ØªØ§Ø¨Ø¹ Ø§ØµÙ„ÛŒ Ø¯Ø± engine/skillStatus.js Ø§Ø³Øª.
        getSkillStatus: getSkillStatusForSkill,

        getPrerequisiteDetails,
        buildSkillPath,
        toggleCategory,

        // --------------------------------
        // Derived
        // --------------------------------

        currentSkills,
        categorizedSkills,
        globalStats,
    };
}

function clamp(value, min, max) {
    return Math.min(Math.max(value, min), max);
}

function ProgressBar({
    value = 0,
    className = "",
    barClassName = "bg-gradient-to-l from-cyan-400 to-blue-500",
}) {
    const safeValue = clamp(Number(value) || 0, 0, 100);

    return (
        <div className={`h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/5 ${className}`}>
            <div
                className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-700 ${barClassName}`}
                style={{ width: `${safeValue}%` }}
            />
        </div>
    );
}

                    <Icon className={`h-4 w-4 ${iconClassName}`} />

            {helper && (

import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { LogIn, Mail, Lock, ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";

import AuthContext from "../context/AuthContext";
import Scene from "../components/ui/Scene";

export default function Login() {
    const navigate = useNavigate();
    const { login, updateUser } = useContext(AuthContext);

    const [form, setForm] = useState({
        email: "",
        password: "",
    });

    const [error, setError] = useState("");
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

    function handleChange(event) {
        const { name, value } = event.target;

        setForm((previous) => ({
            ...previous,
            [name]: value,
        }));
    }

                        "http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/roadmap.php",
                        {
                            method: "POST",
                            headers: {
                                "Content-Type": "application/json",
                            },

    value,

                {label}
            </p>

            <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-black text-white">
                {value}
            </p>

        </div>
    );
}


// ======================================================

// ======================================================

function InfoRow({
    icon,
    label,
    value,
}) {
    return (

                </p>

                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-300">
                    {value}
                </p>

            </div>

        </div>
    );
}

import { useContext, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";

import { questions } from "../data/questions";

import ProgressBar from "../components/quiz/ProgressBar";
import QuestionCard from "../components/quiz/QuestionCard";

import calculateScore from "../engine/calculateScore";
import calculatePercentages from "../engine/calculatePercentages";
import sortByPercentage from "../engine/sortByPercentage";

import QuizContext from "../context/QuizContext";
import Scene from "../components/ui/Scene";

export default function Quiz() {

    const navigate = useNavigate();

    const { setResult } = useContext(QuizContext);

    const [currentQuestion, setCurrentQuestion] = useState(0);

    const [answers, setAnswers] = useState({});

    const question = questions[currentQuestion];

    function handleAnswer(answerIndex) {

        const newAnswers = {
            ...answers,
            [question.id]: answerIndex
        };

        setAnswers(newAnswers);

        if (currentQuestion < questions.length - 1) {

            setTimeout(() => {
                setCurrentQuestion(prev => prev + 1);
            }, 300);

        } else {

            const scores = calculateScore(questions, newAnswers);

            const percentages = calculatePercentages(scores);

            const result = sortByPercentage(percentages);

            setResult(result);

            navigate("/result");

        }

    }

    function previousQuestion() {

        if (currentQuestion > 0) {
            setCurrentQuestion(prev => prev - 1);
        }

    }
    return (
        <section className="relative min-h-screen overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center">

            <Scene variant="quiz" />

            <div className="relative z-10 w-full max-w-2xl px-6 py-8">

                {/* Header + Progress combined */}
                <div className="mb-8">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                        <p className="text-cyan-400/60 text-xs tracking-[8px] font-mono">
                            QUESTION {currentQuestion + 1}/{questions.length}
                        </p>

                        {/* Back button */}
                        {currentQuestion > 0 && (
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setCurrentQuestion(prev => prev - 1)}
                                className="text-cyan-400/60 hover:text-cyan-300 text-sm font-mono tracking-wider transition-colors"
                            >
                                â† BACK
                            </button>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Progress bar */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">

                        {questions.map((_, index) => (

                            <div
                                key={index}
                                className={`
                h-[3px]
                rounded-full
                transition-all
                duration-500

                ${index < currentQuestion
                                        ? `
                            w-10
                            bg-cyan-400
                            shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(34,211,238,.7)]
                          `
                                        : index === currentQuestion
                                            ? `
                            w-14
                            bg-white
                            shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(255,255,255,.8)]
                          `
                                            : `
                            w-6
                            bg-white/10
                          `
                                    }
            `}
                            />

                        ))}

                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Question */}
                <div className="mb-10" dir="rtl">
                    <h1 className="text-white text-2xl md:text-4xl font-bold leading-relaxed tracking-tight">
                        {question.title}
                    </h1>
                </div>

                {/* Options */}
                <div className="space-y-3 mb-8" dir="rtl">
                    {question.answers.map((answer, index) => {
                        const selected = answers[question.id] === index;

                        return (
                            <button
                                key={index}
                                onClick={() => handleAnswer(index)}
                                className={`
                group w-full py-4 px-6
                flex items-center gap-4
                rounded-2xl
                transition-all duration-200
                ${selected
                                        ? 'bg-cyan-500/15 border border-cyan-400/50 shadow-[0_0_25px_rgba(34,211,238,.2)]'
                                        : 'border border-transparent hover:bg-white/[0.03] hover:border-white/10'
                                    }
                `}
                            >
                                {/* Radio indicator */}
                                <div
                                    className={`
                    w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center
                    transition-all flex-shrink-0
                    ${selected
                                            ? 'border-cyan-400 bg-cyan-400/20'
                                            : 'border-white/25 group-hover:border-cyan-400/50'
                                        }
                `}
                                >
                                    {selected && (
                                        <div className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-cyan-400 animate-scale-in" />
                                    )}
                                </div>

                                <span
                                    className={`
                    text-lg transition-all
                    ${selected ? 'text-cyan-100 font-medium' : 'text-white/80'}
                `}
                                >
                                    {answer.text}
                                </span>

                                {/* Checkmark for selected */}
                                {selected && (
                                    <svg
                                        className="w-5 h-5 text-cyan-400 ml-auto animate-fade-in"
                                        fill="none"
                                        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                                        stroke="currentColor"
                                    >
                                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" strokeWidth={2} d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" />
                                    </svg>
                                )}
                            </button>
                        );
                    })}
                </div>

                {/* Footer navigation */}
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">
                    <p className="text-cyan-400/30 text-xs tracking-[4px] font-mono">
                        {answers[question.id] !== undefined ? 'âœ“ ANSWERED' : 'â—‹ PENDING'}
                    </p>

                    {/* Next button - only show if answered */}
                    {answers[question.id] !== undefined && currentQuestion < questions.length - 1 && (
                        <button
                            onClick={() => setCurrentQuestion(prev => prev + 1)}
                            className="text-cyan-400 hover:text-cyan-300 text-sm font-mono tracking-wider transition-colors flex items-center gap-2"
                        >
                            NEXT
                            <svg className="w-4 h-4" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor">

        password: "",
    });

        const { name, value } = event.target;

        setForm((previous) => ({
            ...previous,
            [name]: value,
        }));
    }

                        "http://localhost/devcompass-api/api/roadmap.php",

                            method: "POST",
                            headers: {

                            },

    const { user } = useContext(AuthContext);
    const {
        roadmap,
        progress,
        totalSkills,
        completedSkillsCount,
        roadmapProgress,
    } = useContext(QuizContext);

    const roadmapGoal = user?.roadmap_goal ?? null;
    const [selectedSkill, setSelectedSkill] = useState(null);
    const orderedSkills = roadmap.flatMap((stage) => stage.skills || []);

    const goalTitles = {
        frontend: "Frontend Developer",
        backend: "Backend Developer",
        ai: "AI Developer",
        devops: "DevOps Developer",
        soft: "Soft Skills Developer",
    };

    // --------------------------------
    // Empty roadmap
    // --------------------------------

    if (!roadmap?.length) {
        return (
            <>
                <Scene variant="result" />

                <main className="relative z-10 flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center text-white">
                    <div className="text-center">
                        <div className="text-6xl">
                            ðŸ—ºï¸
                        </div>

                        <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-black">
                            Ù‡Ù†ÙˆØ² Ù†Ù‚Ø´Ù‡ Ø±Ø§Ù‡ÛŒ Ø³Ø§Ø®ØªÙ‡ Ù†Ø´Ø¯Ù‡
                        </h1>
                    </div>
                </main>
            </>
        );
    }

    // --------------------------------
    // Render
    // --------------------------------

    return (
        <>
            <Scene variant="result" />

            <main className="relative z-10 mx-auto max-w-2xl px-6 pb-24 pt-12 text-white">

                {/* Header */}
                <div className="mb-10 space-y-3 text-center">
                    <p className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-[12px] text-cyan-400/60">
                        Your Path
                    </p>

                    <h1 className="text-5xl font-black tracking-tight md:text-6xl">
                        {goalTitles[roadmapGoal] ||
                            "Developer Roadmap"}
                    </h1>
                </div>

                {/* Progress */}
                <section className="mb-12 rounded-3xl border border-white/[0.06] bg-white/[0.02] p-6 backdrop-blur-xl">

                    <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase tracking-[8px] text-cyan-400/70">
                            Progress
                        </span>

                        <span className="text-sm font-mono font-bold text-cyan-400">
                            {roadmapProgress}%
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Progress bar */}
                    <div className="mb-3 h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/[0.04]">
                        <div
                            className="h-full rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 via-cyan-300 to-blue-400 shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(34,211,238,.4)] transition-all duration-700 ease-out"
                            style={{
                                width: `${roadmapProgress}%`,
                            }}
                        />
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

                        <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                            {completedSkillsCount} of{" "}
                            {totalSkills} skills completed
                        </p>

                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                            <div className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-400/60" />

                            <span className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-600">
                                In Progress
                            </span>
                        </div>

                    </div>
                </section>

                {/* Stages */}
                <div className="space-y-16">

                    {roadmap.map((stage) => (
                        <section
                            key={stage.stage}
                            className="space-y-5"
                        >

                            {/* Stage title */}
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">

                                <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-cyan-400/20 bg-cyan-400/[0.08] text-sm font-bold text-cyan-400 backdrop-blur-sm">
                                    {stage.stage}
                                </div>

                                <span className="text-xs font-mono uppercase tracking-[4px] text-zinc-600">
                                    Stage
                                </span>

                            </div>

                            {/* Skills */}
                            <div className="space-y-3">

                                {stage.skills?.map((skill) => (
                                    <SkillCard
                                        key={skill.id}
                                        skill={skill}
                                        status={getSkillStatus(skill, progress, orderedSkills)}
                                        progress={
                                            Number(
                                                progress?.[
                                                    skill.id
                                                ]
                                            ) || 0
                                        }
                                        onClick={
                                            setSelectedSkill
                                        }
                                    />
                                ))}

                            </div>

                        </section>
                    ))}

                </div>
            </main>

            <SkillModal
                skill={selectedSkill}
                onClose={() =>
                    setSelectedSkill(null)
                }
            />
        </>
    );
}

import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import SkillActionButton from "../components/skill/SkillActionButton";
import SkillEstimate from "../components/skill/SkillEstimate";
import SkillExercise from "../components/skill/SkillExercise";
import SkillHeader from "../components/skill/SkillHeader";
import SkillNextSteps from "../components/skill/SkillNextSteps";
import SkillOverallProgress from "../components/skill/SkillOverallProgress";
import SkillPrerequisites from "../components/skill/SkillPrerequisites";
import SkillProgress from "../components/skill/SkillProgress";
import SkillProject from "../components/skill/SkillProject";
import SkillResources from "../components/skill/SkillResources";
import SkillStats from "../components/skill/SkillStats";
import SkillStatus from "../components/skill/SkillStatus";
import SkillSuggestions from "../components/skill/SkillSuggestions";
import Scene from "../components/ui/Scene";
import { useSkillData } from "../hooks/useSkillData";



export default function Skill() {
    const {
        skill,
        skillProgress,
        overallProgress,
        status,
        getButtonText,
        isButtonDisabled,
        estimateEndDate,
        requires,
        progress,
        nextSkillsInStage,
        nextStage,
        currentStage,
        suggestedSkills,
        roadmap,
        diffText,
        handleProgressUpdate,

    } = useSkillData();

    

    if (!skill) {
        return (
            <section className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
                <div className="text-center space-y-4">
                    <span className="text-6xl">ðŸª</span>
                    <h1 className="text-4xl font-black text-white">Ù…Ù‡Ø§Ø±Øª Ù¾ÛŒØ¯Ø§ Ù†Ø´Ø¯</h1>
                    <Link to="/roadmap" className="text-cyan-400 text-sm hover:underline">
                        Ø¨Ø§Ø²Ú¯Ø´Øª Ø¨Ù‡ Ù†Ù‚Ø´Ù‡ Ø±Ø§Ù‡
                    </Link>
                </div>
            </section>
        );
    }

    return (
        <>
            <Scene variant="result" />
            <section className="relative z-10 max-w-2xl mx-auto px-6 pt-16 pb-24 text-white space-y-12">

                <SkillHeader skill={skill} />
                <SkillStatus status={status} />
                <SkillOverallProgress overallProgress={overallProgress} />
                <SkillProgress skillProgress={skillProgress} skillTitle={skill.title} />
                <SkillStats skill={skill} roadmap={roadmap} diffText={diffText} />
                <SkillEstimate estimateEndDate={estimateEndDate} />

                <SkillProject project={skill.project} />
                <SkillExercise exercise={skill.exercise} />

                <SkillPrerequisites requires={requires} progress={progress} />
                <SkillNextSteps
                    nextSkillsInStage={nextSkillsInStage}
                    nextStage={nextStage}
                    currentStage={currentStage}
                />

                <SkillResources resources={skill.resources} />
                <SkillSuggestions
                    suggestedSkills={suggestedSkills}
                    skillProgress={skillProgress}
                />

                <SkillActionButton
                    getButtonText={getButtonText}
                    isButtonDisabled={isButtonDisabled}
                    onProgressUpdate={handleProgressUpdate}

                />
            </section>
        </>
    );
}

import Scene from "../components/ui/Scene";
import { useSkills } from "../hooks/useSkills";

import SkillsHero from "../components/Skills/SkillsHero";
import SkillsContinue from "../components/Skills/SkillsContinue";
import SkillsSearchBar from "../components/Skills/SkillsSearchBar";
import SkillsCategory from "../components/Skills/SkillsCategory";
import SkillsEmptyState from "../components/Skills/SkillsEmptyState";

export default function Skills() {
    const {
        searchTerm,
        setSearchTerm,
        sortBy,
        setSortBy,
        expandedCategories,
        hoveredSkill,
        setHoveredSkill,
        skillsMap,
        getSkillStatus,
        getPrerequisiteDetails,
        buildSkillPath,
        toggleCategory,
        currentSkills,
        categorizedSkills,
        globalStats,
        progress,
    } = useSkills();

    return (
        <>
            <Scene variant="result" />
            <section className="relative z-10 max-w-5xl mx-auto px-6 py-16 text-white space-y-8">
                <SkillsHero globalStats={globalStats} />

                <SkillsContinue
                    currentSkills={currentSkills}
                    getSkillStatus={getSkillStatus}
                    getPrerequisiteDetails={getPrerequisiteDetails}
                    progress={progress}
                    hoveredSkill={hoveredSkill}
                    setHoveredSkill={setHoveredSkill}
                />

                <SkillsSearchBar
                    searchTerm={searchTerm}
                    setSearchTerm={setSearchTerm}
                    sortBy={sortBy}
                    setSortBy={setSortBy}
                />

                <div className="space-y-4">
                    {categorizedSkills.map(category => (
                        <SkillsCategory
                            key={category.id}
                            category={category}
                            isExpanded={expandedCategories[category.id]}
                            onToggle={toggleCategory}
                            skillsMap={skillsMap}
                            getSkillStatus={getSkillStatus}
                            getPrerequisiteDetails={getPrerequisiteDetails}
                            buildSkillPath={buildSkillPath}
                            progress={progress}
                            hoveredSkill={hoveredSkill}
                            setHoveredSkill={setHoveredSkill}
                        />
                    ))}
                </div>

                {categorizedSkills.length === 0 && (
                    <SkillsEmptyState onClearSearch={() => setSearchTerm("")} />
                )}
            </section>
        </>
    );
}
